YOL: english_acquisition_app_current/.github/workflows/main.yml

name: Flutter Windows CI

on:
  push:
    branches:
      - "**"
  pull_request:
    branches:
      - main
      - develop
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

concurrency:
  group: flutter-windows-${{ github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: true

jobs:
  quality-and-windows-build:
    name: Format, Analyze, Test, Windows Release
    runs-on: windows-latest
    timeout-minutes: 45

    steps:
      - name: Checkout repository
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Set up Flutter
        uses: subosito/flutter-action@v2
        with:
          flutter-version: "3.47.9"
          channel: stable
          cache: true

      - name: Show tool versions
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          flutter --version
          dart --version
          git --version

      - name: Enable Windows desktop
        shell: pwsh
        run: flutter config --enable-windows-desktop

      - name: Resolve dependencies
        shell: pwsh
        run: flutter pub get

      - name: Verify formatting
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          dart format --output=none --set-exit-if-changed lib test tool

      - name: Run V7 release audit
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          if (Test-Path ".\tool\v7_release_audit.dart") {
            dart run .\tool\v7_release_audit.dart
          } else {
            Write-Host "V7 audit file is not present in this branch."
          }

      - name: Analyze project
        shell: pwsh
        run: flutter analyze

      - name: Run automated tests
        shell: pwsh
        run: flutter test --reporter expanded

      - name: Build Windows release
        shell: pwsh
        run: flutter build windows --release

      - name: Verify Windows executable
        shell: pwsh
        run: |
          $exe = ".\build\windows\x64\runner\Release\english_acquisition_app.exe"

          if (-not (Test-Path $exe)) {
            throw "Windows Release executable was not created: $exe"
          }

          $file = Get-Item $exe
          Write-Host "Release executable: $($file.FullName)"
          Write-Host "Size: $($file.Length) bytes"
          Write-Host "Created: $($file.LastWriteTime)"

      - name: Upload Windows release artifact
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: english-acquisition-app-windows-${{ github.sha }}
          path: build/windows/x64/runner/Release/**
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 14

      - name: Upload test and diagnostics files
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: english-acquisition-app-evidence-${{ github.sha }}
          path: |
            pubspec.yaml
            pubspec.lock
            analysis_options.yaml
            release/**
          if-no-files-found: warn
          retention-days: 14

if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX)
  set(CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:english_acquisition_app>")
endif()
string(REGEX REPLACE "/$" "" CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}")

# Set the install configuration name.
if(NOT DEFINED CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME)
  if(BUILD_TYPE)
    string(REGEX REPLACE "^[^A-Za-z0-9_]+" ""
           CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "${BUILD_TYPE}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "Release")
  endif()
  message(STATUS "Install configuration: \"${CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME}\"")
endif()

# Set the component getting installed.

  if(COMPONENT)
    message(STATUS "Install component: \"${COMPONENT}\"")
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT "${COMPONENT}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  endif()
endif()

/* This source file must have a .cpp extension so that all C++ compilers
   recognize the extension without flags.  Borland does not know .cxx for
   example.  */
#ifndef __cplusplus
# error "A C compiler has been selected for C++."
#endif

#if !defined(__has_include)
/* If the compiler does not have __has_include, pretend the answer is
   always no.  */
#  define __has_include(x) 0
#endif


/* Version number components: V=Version, R=Revision, P=Patch
   Version date components:   YYYY=Year, MM=Month,   DD=Day  */

#if defined(__INTEL_COMPILER) || defined(__ICC)
# define COMPILER_ID "Intel"
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
#  define SIMULATE_ID "MSVC"
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC__)
#  define SIMULATE_ID "GNU"
# endif
  /* __INTEL_COMPILER = VRP prior to 2021, and then VVVV for 2021 and later,
     except that a few beta releases use the old format with V=2021.  */
# if __INTEL_COMPILER < 2021 || __INTEL_COMPILER == 202110 || __INTEL_COMPILER == 202111
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER/100)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER/10 % 10)
#  if defined(__INTEL_COMPILER_UPDATE)
#   define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER_UPDATE)
#  else
#   define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER   % 10)
#  endif
# else
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER_UPDATE)
   /* The third version component from --version is an update index,
      but no macro is provided for it.  */
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(0)
# endif
# if defined(__INTEL_COMPILER_BUILD_DATE)
   /* __INTEL_COMPILER_BUILD_DATE = YYYYMMDD */
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK DEC(__INTEL_COMPILER_BUILD_DATE)
# endif
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
   /* _MSC_VER = VVRR */
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(_MSC_VER / 100)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(_MSC_VER % 100)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC__)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUC__)
# elif defined(__GNUG__)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUG__)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC_MINOR__)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__GNUC_MINOR__)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
# endif

#elif (defined(__clang__) && defined(__INTEL_CLANG_COMPILER)) || defined(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER)
# define COMPILER_ID "IntelLLVM"
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
# define SIMULATE_ID "MSVC"
#endif
#if defined(__GNUC__)
# define SIMULATE_ID "GNU"
#endif
/* __INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER = VVVVRP prior to 2021.2.0, VVVVRRPP for 2021.2.0 and
 * later.  Look for 6 digit vs. 8 digit version number to decide encoding.
 * VVVV is no smaller than the current year when a version is released.
 */
#if __INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER < 1000000L
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER/100)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER/10 % 10)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER    % 10)
#else
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER/10000)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER/100 % 100)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__INTEL_LLVM_COMPILER     % 100)
#endif
#if defined(_MSC_VER)
  /* _MSC_VER = VVRR */
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(_MSC_VER / 100)
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(_MSC_VER % 100)
#endif
#if defined(__GNUC__)
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUC__)
#elif defined(__GNUG__)
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUG__)
#endif
#if defined(__GNUC_MINOR__)
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__GNUC_MINOR__)
#endif
#if defined(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
# define SIMULATE_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#endif

#elif defined(__PATHCC__)
# define COMPILER_ID "PathScale"
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__PATHCC__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__PATHCC_MINOR__)
# if defined(__PATHCC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__PATHCC_PATCHLEVEL__)
# endif

#elif defined(__BORLANDC__) && defined(__CODEGEARC_VERSION__)
# define COMPILER_ID "Embarcadero"
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR HEX(__CODEGEARC_VERSION__>>24 & 0x00FF)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR HEX(__CODEGEARC_VERSION__>>16 & 0x00FF)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__CODEGEARC_VERSION__     & 0xFFFF)

#elif defined(__BORLANDC__)

# define COMPILER_ID "AppleClang"
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
#  define SIMULATE_ID "MSVC"
# endif
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__clang_major__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__clang_minor__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__clang_patchlevel__)
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
   /* _MSC_VER = VVRR */
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(_MSC_VER / 100)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(_MSC_VER % 100)
# endif
# define COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK DEC(__apple_build_version__)

#elif defined(__clang__) && defined(__ARMCOMPILER_VERSION)
# define COMPILER_ID "ARMClang"
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__ARMCOMPILER_VERSION/1000000)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__ARMCOMPILER_VERSION/10000 % 100)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__ARMCOMPILER_VERSION/100   % 100)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL DEC(__ARMCOMPILER_VERSION)

#elif defined(__clang__) && defined(__ti__)
# define COMPILER_ID "TIClang"
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__ti_major__)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__ti_minor__)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__ti_patchlevel__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL DEC(__ti_version__)

#elif defined(__clang__)
# define COMPILER_ID "Clang"
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
#  define SIMULATE_ID "MSVC"
# endif
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__clang_major__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__clang_minor__)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__clang_patchlevel__)
# if defined(_MSC_VER)
   /* _MSC_VER = VVRR */
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(_MSC_VER / 100)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(_MSC_VER % 100)
# endif

#elif defined(__LCC__) && (defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__GNUG__) || defined(__MCST__))
# define COMPILER_ID "LCC"
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__LCC__ / 100)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__LCC__ % 100)
# if defined(__LCC_MINOR__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__LCC_MINOR__)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC__) && defined(__GNUC_MINOR__)
#  define SIMULATE_ID "GNU"
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUC__)
#  define SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__GNUC_MINOR__)
#  if defined(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#   define SIMULATE_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#  endif
# endif

#elif defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__GNUG__)
# define COMPILER_ID "GNU"
# if defined(__GNUC__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUC__)
# else
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__GNUG__)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC_MINOR__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__GNUC_MINOR__)
# endif
# if defined(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__GNUC_PATCHLEVEL__)
# endif

#elif defined(_MSC_VER)
# define COMPILER_ID "MSVC"
  /* _MSC_VER = VVRR */
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(_MSC_VER / 100)
# define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(_MSC_VER % 100)
# if defined(_MSC_FULL_VER)
#  if _MSC_VER >= 1400
    /* _MSC_FULL_VER = VVRRPPPPP */
#   define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(_MSC_FULL_VER % 100000)
#  else
    /* _MSC_FULL_VER = VVRRPPPP */
#   define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(_MSC_FULL_VER % 10000)
#  endif
# endif
# if defined(_MSC_BUILD)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK DEC(_MSC_BUILD)
# endif

#elif defined(_ADI_COMPILER)
# define COMPILER_ID "ADSP"
#if defined(__VERSIONNUM__)
  /* __VERSIONNUM__ = 0xVVRRPPTT */
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__VERSIONNUM__ >> 24 & 0xFF)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__VERSIONNUM__ >> 16 & 0xFF)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__VERSIONNUM__ >> 8 & 0xFF)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK DEC(__VERSIONNUM__ & 0xFF)
#endif

#elif defined(__IAR_SYSTEMS_ICC__) || defined(__IAR_SYSTEMS_ICC)
# define COMPILER_ID "IAR"
# if defined(__VER__) && defined(__ICCARM__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC((__VER__) / 1000000)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(((__VER__) / 1000) % 1000)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC((__VER__) % 1000)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL DEC(__IAR_SYSTEMS_ICC__)
# elif defined(__VER__) && (defined(__ICCAVR__) || defined(__ICCRX__) || defined(__ICCRH850__) || defined(__ICCRL78__) || defined(__ICC430__) || defined(__ICCRISCV__) || defined(__ICCV850__) || defined(__ICC8051__) || defined(__ICCSTM8__))
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC((__VER__) / 100)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC((__VER__) - (((__VER__) / 100)*100))
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__SUBVERSION__)
#  define COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL DEC(__IAR_SYSTEMS_ICC__)
# endif

#elif defined(__DCC__) && defined(_DIAB_TOOL)
# define COMPILER_ID "Diab"
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR DEC(__VERSION_MAJOR_NUMBER__)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR DEC(__VERSION_MINOR_NUMBER__)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH DEC(__VERSION_ARCH_FEATURE_NUMBER__)
  # define COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK DEC(__VERSION_BUG_FIX_NUMBER__)



/* These compilers are either not known or too old to define an
  identification macro.  Try to identify the platform and guess that
  it is the native compiler.  */
#elif defined(__hpux) || defined(__hpua)
# define COMPILER_ID "HP"

#else /* unknown compiler */
# define COMPILER_ID ""
#endif

/* Construct the string literal in pieces to prevent the source from
   getting matched.  Store it in a pointer rather than an array
   because some compilers will just produce instructions to fill the
   array rather than assigning a pointer to a static array.  */
char const* info_compiler = "INFO" ":" "compiler[" COMPILER_ID "]";
#ifdef SIMULATE_ID
char const* info_simulate = "INFO" ":" "simulate[" SIMULATE_ID "]";
#endif

#ifdef __QNXNTO__
char const* qnxnto = "INFO" ":" "qnxnto[]";
#endif

#if defined(__CRAYXT_COMPUTE_LINUX_TARGET)
char const *info_cray = "INFO" ":" "compiler_wrapper[CrayPrgEnv]";
#endif

#define STRINGIFY_HELPER(X) #X
#define STRINGIFY(X) STRINGIFY_HELPER(X)

/* Identify known platforms by name.  */
#if defined(__linux) || defined(__linux__) || defined(linux)

#elif defined(__MSYS__)

/* For windows compilers MSVC and Intel we can determine
   the architecture of the compiler being used.  This is because
   the compilers do not have flags that can change the architecture,
   but rather depend on which compiler is being used
*/
#if defined(_WIN32) && defined(_MSC_VER)
# if defined(_M_IA64)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "IA64"

# elif defined(_M_ARM64EC)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "ARM64EC"

# elif defined(_M_X64) || defined(_M_AMD64)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "x64"

# elif defined(_M_IX86)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "X86"

# elif defined(_M_ARM64)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "ARM64"

# elif defined(_M_ARM)
#  if _M_ARM == 4
#   define ARCHITECTURE_ID "ARMV4I"
#  elif _M_ARM == 5
#   define ARCHITECTURE_ID "ARMV5I"
#  else
#   define ARCHITECTURE_ID "ARMV" STRINGIFY(_M_ARM)
#  endif

# elif defined(_M_MIPS)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "MIPS"

# elif defined(_M_SH)
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID "SHx"

# else /* unknown architecture */
#  define ARCHITECTURE_ID ""
# endif

#elif defined(__WATCOMC__)
# if defined(_M_I86)

# elif defined(_M_IX86)

#elif defined(__ghs__)

/* Convert integer to decimal digit literals.  */
#define DEC(n)                   \
  ('0' + (((n) / 10000000)%10)), \
  ('0' + (((n) / 1000000)%10)),  \
  ('0' + (((n) / 100000)%10)),   \
  ('0' + (((n) / 10000)%10)),    \
  ('0' + (((n) / 1000)%10)),     \
  ('0' + (((n) / 100)%10)),      \
  ('0' + (((n) / 10)%10)),       \
  ('0' +  ((n) % 10))

/* Convert integer to hex digit literals.  */
#define HEX(n)             \
  ('0' + ((n)>>28 & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>24 & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>20 & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>16 & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>12 & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>8  & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)>>4  & 0xF)), \
  ('0' + ((n)     & 0xF))

/* Construct a string literal encoding the version number. */
#ifdef COMPILER_VERSION
char const* info_version = "INFO" ":" "compiler_version[" COMPILER_VERSION "]";

/* Construct a string literal encoding the version number components. */
#elif defined(COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR)
char const info_version[] = {
  'I', 'N', 'F', 'O', ':',
  'c','o','m','p','i','l','e','r','_','v','e','r','s','i','o','n','[',
  COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR,
# ifdef COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR
  '.', COMPILER_VERSION_MINOR,
#  ifdef COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH
   '.', COMPILER_VERSION_PATCH,
#   ifdef COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK
    '.', COMPILER_VERSION_TWEAK,
#   endif
#  endif
# endif
  ']','\0'};
#endif

/* Construct a string literal encoding the internal version number. */
#ifdef COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL
char const info_version_internal[] = {
  'I', 'N', 'F', 'O', ':',
  'c','o','m','p','i','l','e','r','_','v','e','r','s','i','o','n','_',
  'i','n','t','e','r','n','a','l','[',
  COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL,']','\0'};
#elif defined(COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL_STR)
char const* info_version_internal = "INFO" ":" "compiler_version_internal[" COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL_STR "]";
#endif

/* Construct a string literal encoding the version number components. */
#ifdef SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR
char const info_simulate_version[] = {
  'I', 'N', 'F', 'O', ':',
  's','i','m','u','l','a','t','e','_','v','e','r','s','i','o','n','[',
  SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR,
# ifdef SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR
  '.', SIMULATE_VERSION_MINOR,
#  ifdef SIMULATE_VERSION_PATCH
   '.', SIMULATE_VERSION_PATCH,
#   ifdef SIMULATE_VERSION_TWEAK
    '.', SIMULATE_VERSION_TWEAK,
#   endif
#  endif
# endif
  ']','\0'};
#endif

/* Construct the string literal in pieces to prevent the source from
   getting matched.  Store it in a pointer rather than an array
   because some compilers will just produce instructions to fill the
   array rather than assigning a pointer to a static array.  */
char const* info_platform = "INFO" ":" "platform[" PLATFORM_ID "]";
char const* info_arch = "INFO" ":" "arch[" ARCHITECTURE_ID "]";



#define CXX_STD_98 199711L
#define CXX_STD_11 201103L
#define CXX_STD_14 201402L
#define CXX_STD_17 201703L
#define CXX_STD_20 202002L
#define CXX_STD_23 202302L

#if defined(__INTEL_COMPILER) && defined(_MSVC_LANG)
#  if _MSVC_LANG > CXX_STD_17
#    define CXX_STD _MSVC_LANG
#  elif _MSVC_LANG == CXX_STD_17 && defined(__cpp_aggregate_paren_init)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_20
#  elif _MSVC_LANG > CXX_STD_14 && __cplusplus > CXX_STD_17
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_20
#  elif _MSVC_LANG > CXX_STD_14
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_17
#  elif defined(__INTEL_CXX11_MODE__) && defined(__cpp_aggregate_nsdmi)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_14
#  elif defined(__INTEL_CXX11_MODE__)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_11
#  else
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_98
#  endif
#elif defined(_MSC_VER) && defined(_MSVC_LANG)
#  if _MSVC_LANG > __cplusplus
#    define CXX_STD _MSVC_LANG
#  else
#    define CXX_STD __cplusplus
#  endif
#elif defined(__NVCOMPILER)
#  if __cplusplus == CXX_STD_17 && defined(__cpp_aggregate_paren_init)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_20
#  else
#    define CXX_STD __cplusplus
#  endif
#elif defined(__INTEL_COMPILER) || defined(__PGI)
#  if __cplusplus == CXX_STD_11 && defined(__cpp_namespace_attributes)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_17
#  elif __cplusplus == CXX_STD_11 && defined(__cpp_aggregate_nsdmi)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_14
#  else
#    define CXX_STD __cplusplus
#  endif
#elif (defined(__IBMCPP__) || defined(__ibmxl__)) && defined(__linux__)
#  if __cplusplus == CXX_STD_11 && defined(__cpp_aggregate_nsdmi)
#    define CXX_STD CXX_STD_14
#  else
#    define CXX_STD __cplusplus
#  endif
#elif __cplusplus == 1 && defined(__GXX_EXPERIMENTAL_CXX0X__)
#  define CXX_STD CXX_STD_11
#else
#  define CXX_STD __cplusplus
#endif

const char* info_language_standard_default = "INFO" ":" "standard_default["
#if CXX_STD > CXX_STD_23
  "26"
#elif CXX_STD > CXX_STD_20
  "23"
#elif CXX_STD > CXX_STD_17
  "20"
#elif CXX_STD > CXX_STD_14
  "17"
#elif CXX_STD > CXX_STD_11
  "14"
#elif CXX_STD >= CXX_STD_11
  "11"
#else
  "98"
#endif
"]";

const char* info_language_extensions_default = "INFO" ":" "extensions_default["
#if (defined(__clang__) || defined(__GNUC__) || defined(__xlC__) ||           \
     defined(__TI_COMPILER_VERSION__) || defined(__RENESAS__)) &&             \
  !defined(__STRICT_ANSI__)
  "ON"
#else
  "OFF"
#endif
"]";

/*--------------------------------------------------------------------------*/

int main(int argc, char* argv[])
{
  int require = 0;
  require += info_compiler[argc];
  require += info_platform[argc];
  require += info_arch[argc];
#ifdef COMPILER_VERSION_MAJOR
  require += info_version[argc];
#endif
#if defined(COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL) || defined(COMPILER_VERSION_INTERNAL_STR)
  require += info_version_internal[argc];
#endif
#ifdef SIMULATE_ID
  require += info_simulate[argc];
#endif
#ifdef SIMULATE_VERSION_MAJOR
  require += info_simulate_version[argc];
#endif
#if defined(__CRAYXT_COMPUTE_LINUX_TARGET)
  require += info_cray[argc];
#endif
  require += info_language_standard_default[argc];
  require += info_language_extensions_default[argc];
  (void)argv;
  return require;
}

endif()

  if(BUILD_TYPE)
    string(REGEX REPLACE "^[^A-Za-z0-9_]+" ""
           CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "${BUILD_TYPE}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME "Release")
  endif()
  message(STATUS "Install configuration: \"${CMAKE_INSTALL_CONFIG_NAME}\"")
endif()

if(NOT CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  if(COMPONENT)
    message(STATUS "Install component: \"${COMPONENT}\"")
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT "${COMPONENT}")
  else()
    set(CMAKE_INSTALL_COMPONENT)
  endif()
endif()

  set(CMAKE_CROSSCOMPILING "FALSE")
endif()

string(REPLACE ";" "\n" CMAKE_INSTALL_MANIFEST_CONTENT
       "${CMAKE_INSTALL_MANIFEST_FILES}")

  file(WRITE "C:/Users/forma/Desktop/Projects/english_acquisition_app_current/build/windows/x64/flutter/install_local_manifest.txt"
     "${CMAKE_INSTALL_MANIFEST_CONTENT}")

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import 'routing/app_router.dart';
import 'theme/app_theme.dart';

final class EnglishAcquisitionApp extends ConsumerWidget {
  const EnglishAcquisitionApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) => MaterialApp.router(
    debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
    routerConfig: ref.watch(appRouterProvider),
    theme: AppTheme.light,
    title: 'English Acquisition',
  );
}

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';

import '../core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import '../features/authentication/authentication.dart';
import '../features/goals/application/controllers/goals_controller.dart';
import '../features/goals/data/repositories/sqlite_goals_repository.dart';
import '../features/learner_profile/application/controllers/learner_profile_controller.dart';
import '../features/learner_profile/data/repositories/sqlite_learner_profile_repository.dart';
import '../features/learning_session/application/learning_session_controller.dart';
import '../features/learning_session/learning_session.dart';

final localDatabaseProvider = FutureProvider<LocalDatabase>((ref) async {
  final database = await LocalDatabase.open();
  ref.onDispose(database.close);
  return database;
});

final learnerProfileControllerProvider =
    FutureProvider.autoDispose<LearnerProfileController>((ref) async {
      final user = ref.watch(authenticationControllerProvider).user;
      if (user == null) throw StateError('Authentication is required.');
      final database = await ref.watch(localDatabaseProvider.future);
      final controller = LearnerProfileController(
        SqliteLearnerProfileRepository(database),
        user.id,
      );
      ref.onDispose(controller.dispose);
      await controller.load();
      return controller;
    });

final goalsControllerProvider = FutureProvider.autoDispose<GoalsController>((
  ref,
) async {
  final user = ref.watch(authenticationControllerProvider).user;
  if (user == null) throw StateError('Authentication is required.');
  final database = await ref.watch(localDatabaseProvider.future);
  final controller = GoalsController(SqliteGoalsRepository(database), user.id);
  ref.onDispose(controller.dispose);
  await controller.load();
  return controller;
});

final learningSessionControllerProvider =
    FutureProvider.autoDispose<LearningSessionController>((ref) async {
      final user = ref.watch(authenticationControllerProvider).user;
      if (user == null) throw StateError('Authentication is required.');
      final database = await ref.watch(localDatabaseProvider.future);
      final controller = LearningSessionController(
        LearningSessionRepository(database),
        user.id,
      );
      ref.onDispose(controller.dispose);
      controller.load();
      return controller;
    });

import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../features/authentication/authentication.dart';
import '../../features/goals/presentation/pages/goal_editor_page.dart';
import '../../features/goals/presentation/pages/goals_page.dart';
import '../../features/home/home.dart';
import '../../features/learner_profile/presentation/pages/learner_profile_edit_page.dart';
import '../../features/learner_profile/presentation/pages/learner_profile_page.dart';
import '../../features/learning_session/presentation/learning_insights_page.dart';
import '../../features/learning_session/presentation/learning_plan_page.dart';
import '../../features/learning_session/presentation/learning_session_page.dart';
import '../../features/learning_session/presentation/progress_page.dart';
import '../../features/simulation/presentation/simulation_lab_page.dart';

final appRouterProvider = Provider<GoRouter>((ref) {
  final authentication = ref.watch(authenticationControllerProvider);

  return GoRouter(
    // V7 security policy requires an explicit PIN after every process start.
    // Starting directly on the sign-in route also removes the possibility of
    // an indefinite startup progress indicator.
    initialLocation: '/sign-in',
    refreshListenable: authentication,
    redirect: (context, state) {
      final isSigningIn = state.matchedLocation == '/sign-in';
      final isAuthenticated =
          authentication.status == AuthenticationStatus.authenticated;

      if (!isAuthenticated) {
        return isSigningIn ? null : '/sign-in';
      }

      if (isSigningIn) {
        return '/home';
      }

      return null;
    },
    routes: <RouteBase>[
      GoRoute(
        path: '/sign-in',
        builder: (context, state) => const SignInPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/simulations',
        builder: (context, state) => const SimulationLabPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/simulation/:id',
        builder: (context, state) =>
            SimulationRoomPage(id: state.pathParameters['id']!),
      ),
      GoRoute(path: '/home', builder: (context, state) => const HomePage()),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/profile',
        builder: (context, state) => const LearnerProfilePage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/profile/edit',
        builder: (context, state) => const LearnerProfileEditPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(path: '/goals', builder: (context, state) => const GoalsPage()),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/learning-plan',
        builder: (context, state) => const LearningPlanPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/insights',
        builder: (context, state) => const LearningInsightsPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/progress',
        builder: (context, state) => const ProgressPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/session',
        builder: (context, state) => const LearningSessionPage(),
      ),
      GoRoute(
        path: '/goals/edit',
        builder: (context, state) =>
            GoalEditorPage(goalId: state.uri.queryParameters['id']),
      ),
    ],
  );
});

BOYUT: 961 bayt

import 'dart:io';

final class LocalBackupService {
  const LocalBackupService();

  Future<File?> createDatabaseBackup({
    required File databaseFile,
    required Directory backupDirectory,
    DateTime? now,
  }) async {
    if (!await databaseFile.exists()) return null;
    await backupDirectory.create(recursive: true);
    final stamp = (now ?? DateTime.now().toUtc())
        .toIso8601String()
        .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), '')
        .substring(0, 14);
    return databaseFile.copy(
      '${backupDirectory.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}'
      'english_acquisition_$stamp.sqlite3.bak',
    );
  }

  Future<void> restoreDatabaseBackup({
    required File backupFile,
    required File databaseFile,
  }) async {
    if (!await backupFile.exists()) {
      throw StateError('Backup file does not exist: ${backupFile.path}');
    }
    await databaseFile.parent.create(recursive: true);
    await backupFile.copy(databaseFile.path);
  }
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:sqlite3/sqlite3.dart';

final class LocalDatabase {
  LocalDatabase._(this._database);
  static const currentSchemaVersion = 3;
  final Database _database;
  Database get raw => _database;

  static Future<LocalDatabase> open() async {
    final appData = Platform.environment['APPDATA'] ?? Directory.current.path;
    final data = Directory(
      '$appData${Platform.pathSeparator}EnglishAcquisitionApp${Platform.pathSeparator}data',
    );
    await data.create(recursive: true);
    final value = LocalDatabase._(
      sqlite3.open(
        '${data.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}english_acquisition.sqlite3',
      ),
    );
    value._migrate();
    return value;
  }

  static LocalDatabase inMemory() {
    final value = LocalDatabase._(sqlite3.openInMemory());
    value._migrate();
    return value;
  }

  void _migrate() {
    _database.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON');
    _database.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
    try {
      var version = _database.userVersion;
      if (version == 0) {
        _createPhase2Schema();
        _database.userVersion = 1;
        version = 1;
      }
      if (version == 1) {
        _createPhase3Schema();
        _database.userVersion = 2;
        version = 2;
      }
      if (version == 2) {
        _createAdaptiveSchema();
        _database.userVersion = 3;
        version = 3;
      }
      if (version != currentSchemaVersion) {
        throw StateError('Unsupported database schema version: $version');
      }
      _database.execute('COMMIT');
    } catch (_) {
      _database.execute('ROLLBACK');
      rethrow;
    }
  }

  void _createPhase2Schema() {
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learner_profiles (learner_id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, display_name TEXT NOT NULL, native_language TEXT NOT NULL, english_level TEXT NOT NULL, occupational_role TEXT NOT NULL, industry TEXT NOT NULL, procurement_experience_years INTEGER NOT NULL, communication_tasks TEXT NOT NULL, preferred_session_minutes INTEGER NOT NULL, accessibility_notes TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL) STRICT",
    );
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learner_goals (id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, learner_id TEXT NOT NULL, title TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL, occupational_context TEXT NOT NULL, priority INTEGER NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, target_date TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL) STRICT",
    );
    _database.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_goals_learner_priority ON learner_goals (learner_id, status, priority, updated_at)',
    );
  }

  void _createPhase3Schema() {
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS learning_sessions (id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, learner_id TEXT NOT NULL, focus TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('active','completed')), current_step INTEGER NOT NULL, total_steps INTEGER NOT NULL, started_at TEXT NOT NULL, completed_at TEXT, updated_at TEXT NOT NULL) STRICT",
    );
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS practice_responses (id TEXT NOT NULL PRIMARY KEY, learner_id TEXT NOT NULL, session_id TEXT NOT NULL, activity_key TEXT NOT NULL, activity_type TEXT NOT NULL, response_text TEXT NOT NULL, is_correct INTEGER, created_at TEXT NOT NULL, FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES learning_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE) STRICT",
    );
    _database.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_sessions_learner_status ON learning_sessions (learner_id, status, updated_at)',
    );
    _database.execute(
      'CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_responses_learner_session ON practice_responses (learner_id, session_id, created_at)',
    );
  }

  void _createAdaptiveSchema() {
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS activity_mastery (learner_id TEXT NOT NULL, activity_key TEXT NOT NULL, skill TEXT NOT NULL, attempts INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, mastery REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, next_review_at TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(learner_id, activity_key)) STRICT",
    );
    _database.execute(
      "CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS session_activities (session_id TEXT NOT NULL, learner_id TEXT NOT NULL, position INTEGER NOT NULL, activity_key TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(session_id, position), FOREIGN KEY(session_id) REFERENCES learning_sessions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE) STRICT",
    );
  }

  void close() => _database.close();
}

import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';
import 'package:crypto/crypto.dart';

final class PinHasher {
  const PinHasher({this.iterations = 120000});
  final int iterations;

  String createSalt() {
    final random = Random.secure();
    return base64UrlEncode(List<int>.generate(24, (_) => random.nextInt(256)));
  }

  String hash({required String pin, required String salt}) {
    var value = Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode('$salt:$pin'));
    final key = utf8.encode(salt);
    for (var index = 0; index < iterations; index++) {
      value = Uint8List.fromList(Hmac(sha256, key).convert(value).bytes);
    }
    return base64UrlEncode(value);
  }

  bool verify({
    required String expectedHash,
    required String pin,
    required String salt,
  }) {
    final actual = hash(pin: pin, salt: salt);
    if (actual.length != expectedHash.length) return false;
    var difference = 0;
    for (var index = 0; index < actual.length; index++) {
      difference |= actual.codeUnitAt(index) ^ expectedHash.codeUnitAt(index);
    }
    return difference == 0;
  }
}

import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';

final class StoredCredential {
  const StoredCredential({
    required this.failedAttempts,
    required this.hash,
    required this.salt,
    this.lockedUntilUtc,
  });

  final int failedAttempts;
  final String hash;
  final DateTime? lockedUntilUtc;
  final String salt;
}

abstract interface class SecureCredentialStore {
  Future<StoredCredential?> read(String userId);
  Future<void> write(String userId, StoredCredential credential);
}

final class FlutterSecureCredentialStore implements SecureCredentialStore {
  const FlutterSecureCredentialStore(this._storage);
  final FlutterSecureStorage _storage;

  String _key(String userId, String field) => 'credential_${userId}_$field';

  @override
  Future<StoredCredential?> read(String userId) async {
    final hash = await _storage.read(key: _key(userId, 'hash'));
    final salt = await _storage.read(key: _key(userId, 'salt'));
    if (hash == null || salt == null) return null;
    final failed =
        int.tryParse(await _storage.read(key: _key(userId, 'failed')) ?? '') ??
        0;
    final lockedRaw = await _storage.read(key: _key(userId, 'locked_until'));
    return StoredCredential(
      failedAttempts: failed,
      hash: hash,
      salt: salt,
      lockedUntilUtc: lockedRaw == null ? null : DateTime.tryParse(lockedRaw),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> write(String userId, StoredCredential credential) async {
    await _storage.write(key: _key(userId, 'hash'), value: credential.hash);
    await _storage.write(key: _key(userId, 'salt'), value: credential.salt);
    await _storage.write(
      key: _key(userId, 'failed'),
      value: credential.failedAttempts.toString(),
    );
    if (credential.lockedUntilUtc == null) {
      await _storage.delete(key: _key(userId, 'locked_until'));
    } else {
      await _storage.write(
        key: _key(userId, 'locked_until'),
        value: credential.lockedUntilUtc!.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      );
    }
  }
}

abstract interface class SecureSessionStore {
  Future<void> clear();
  Future<String?> readUserId();
  Future<void> writeUserId(String userId);
}

/// Keeps the unlocked profile only in process memory.
///
/// The desktop app deliberately requires the PIN after every restart. Persisting
/// the active profile in Windows secure storage added no security value and
/// allowed a concurrent startup delete to race with a successful sign-in write.
/// PIN credentials remain protected by the credential store; only the transient
/// active-profile marker is kept here.
final class VolatileSecureSessionStore implements SecureSessionStore {
  String? _userId;

  @override
  Future<void> clear() async {
    _userId = null;
  }

  @override
  Future<String?> readUserId() async => _userId;

  @override
  Future<void> writeUserId(String userId) async {
    _userId = userId;
  }
}

import '../learning_session/curriculum.dart';

final class CoachFeedback {
  const CoachFeedback(
    this.score,
    this.message,
    this.strengths,
    this.targets,
    this.model,
  );
  final int score;
  final String message, model;
  final List<String> strengths, targets;
}

final class OfflineProcurementAiCoach {
  const OfflineProcurementAiCoach();
  CoachFeedback evaluate(CurriculumActivity a, String response) {
    final t = response.trim(),
        lower = t.toLowerCase(),
        strengths = <String>[],
        targets = <String>[];
    if (t.length >= 15) {
      strengths.add('Complete response.');
    } else {
      targets.add('Write a complete sentence.');
    }
    if (RegExp(r'please|could you|would you').hasMatch(lower)) {
      strengths.add('Professional tone.');
    } else if (a.kind == ActivityKind.writing ||
        a.kind == ActivityKind.simulation) {
      targets.add('Use a polite request phrase.');
    }
    final exact = a.answer.isNotEmpty && _n(t).contains(_n(a.answer));
    final score =
        ((exact ? 85 : 55) + strengths.length * 5 - targets.length * 5)
            .clamp(0, 100)
            .toInt();
    return CoachFeedback(
      score,
      score >= 75 ? 'Strong response.' : 'Good attempt. Apply the feedback.',
      strengths,
      targets,
      a.model,
    );
  }

  String _n(String s) => s
      .toLowerCase()
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^a-z0-9 ]'), '')
      .replaceAll(RegExp(r'\s+'), ' ')
      .trim();
}

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../domain/entities/authenticated_user.dart';
import '../../domain/failures/authentication_failure.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/authentication_repository.dart';
import '../../domain/value_objects/authentication_status.dart';

final class AuthenticationController extends ChangeNotifier {
  AuthenticationController(this._repository);

  static const Duration _sessionClearTimeout = Duration(seconds: 2);

  final AuthenticationRepository _repository;
  bool _disposed = false;

  String? errorMessage;
  bool isBusy = false;
  AuthenticationStatus status = AuthenticationStatus.initializing;
  AuthenticatedUser? user;

  /// Unlocks the startup UI immediately and clears any stale persisted session
  /// in the background. The app intentionally asks for a PIN after every
  /// process start, so secure-storage latency must never block the first frame.
  Future<void> initialize() async {
    user = null;
    errorMessage = null;
    isBusy = false;
    status = AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated;
    _notifySafely();

    unawaited(_clearPersistedSessionSafely());
  }

  Future<void> _clearPersistedSessionSafely() async {
    try {
      await _repository.signOut().timeout(_sessionClearTimeout);
    } catch (_) {
      // A stale session identifier is never trusted at startup. Failure to
      // clear secure storage must not trap the user on an endless spinner.
    }
  }

  Future<void> signIn({
    required String password,
    required String userName,
  }) async {
    errorMessage = null;
    isBusy = true;
    _notifySafely();

    try {
      user = await _repository.signIn(password: password, userName: userName);
      status = AuthenticationStatus.authenticated;
    } on AuthenticationFailure catch (failure) {
      errorMessage = failure.message;
      status = AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated;
    } catch (_) {
      errorMessage = 'Sign-in could not be completed.';
      status = AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated;
    } finally {
      isBusy = false;
      _notifySafely();
    }
  }

  Future<void> signOut() async {
    user = null;
    errorMessage = null;
    isBusy = false;
    status = AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated;
    _notifySafely();

    try {
      await _repository.signOut().timeout(_sessionClearTimeout);
    } catch (_) {
      // The in-memory session is already locked. Secure-storage cleanup can be
      // retried on the next launch without keeping the UI blocked.
    }
  }

  void _notifySafely() {
    if (!_disposed) {
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    super.dispose();
  }
}

import '../../../../core/security/pin_hasher.dart';
import '../../../../core/security/secure_credential_store.dart';
import '../../../../core/security/secure_session_store.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/authenticated_user.dart';
import '../../domain/failures/authentication_failure.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/authentication_repository.dart';

final class LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository
    implements AuthenticationRepository {
  LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
    this._sessionStore,
    this._credentialStore, {
    PinHasher pinHasher = const PinHasher(),
    DateTime Function()? clock,
  }) : _pinHasher = pinHasher,
       _clock = clock ?? DateTime.now;

  static const maxFailedAttempts = 5;
  static const lockDuration = Duration(minutes: 5);

  final SecureCredentialStore _credentialStore;
  final DateTime Function() _clock;
  final PinHasher _pinHasher;
  final SecureSessionStore _sessionStore;

  static const users = <String, AuthenticatedUser>{
    'USER_1': AuthenticatedUser(displayName: 'Furkan', id: 'learner-1'),
    'USER_2': AuthenticatedUser(displayName: 'Learner 2', id: 'learner-2'),
  };

  @override
  Future<AuthenticatedUser?> restore() async {
    final userId = await _sessionStore.readUserId();
    for (final user in users.values) {
      if (user.id == userId) return user;
    }
    return null;
  }

  @override
  Future<AuthenticatedUser> signIn({
    required String password,
    required String userName,
  }) async {
    final user = users[userName.trim().toUpperCase()];
    if (user == null) {
      throw const AuthenticationFailure('Select USER_1 or USER_2.');
    }
    if (!RegExp(r'^\d{6}$').hasMatch(password)) {
      throw const AuthenticationFailure('PIN must contain exactly six digits.');
    }

    final now = _clock().toUtc();
    var credential = await _credentialStore.read(user.id);
    if (credential == null) {
      final salt = _pinHasher.createSalt();
      credential = StoredCredential(
        failedAttempts: 0,
        hash: _pinHasher.hash(pin: password, salt: salt),
        salt: salt,
      );
      await _credentialStore.write(user.id, credential);
      await _sessionStore.writeUserId(user.id);
      return user;
    }

    final lockedUntil = credential.lockedUntilUtc;
    if (lockedUntil != null && now.isBefore(lockedUntil)) {
      final seconds = lockedUntil.difference(now).inSeconds + 1;
      throw AuthenticationFailure(
        'Profile locked. Try again in $seconds seconds.',
      );
    }

    if (!_pinHasher.verify(
      expectedHash: credential.hash,
      pin: password,
      salt: credential.salt,
    )) {
      final failures = credential.failedAttempts + 1;
      final shouldLock = failures >= maxFailedAttempts;
      await _credentialStore.write(
        user.id,
        StoredCredential(
          failedAttempts: shouldLock ? 0 : failures,
          hash: credential.hash,
          salt: credential.salt,
          lockedUntilUtc: shouldLock ? now.add(lockDuration) : null,
        ),
      );
      if (shouldLock) {
        throw const AuthenticationFailure(
          'Too many incorrect attempts. Profile locked for five minutes.',
        );
      }
      throw AuthenticationFailure(
        'Incorrect PIN. ${maxFailedAttempts - failures} attempts remaining.',
      );
    }

    await _credentialStore.write(
      user.id,
      StoredCredential(
        failedAttempts: 0,
        hash: credential.hash,
        salt: credential.salt,
      ),
    );
    await _sessionStore.writeUserId(user.id);
    return user;
  }

  @override
  Future<void> signOut() => _sessionStore.clear();
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../domain/entities/learner_goal.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/goals_repository.dart';

final class GoalsController extends ChangeNotifier {
  GoalsController(this._repository, this.learnerId);

  final GoalsRepository _repository;
  final String learnerId;

  String? errorMessage;
  List<LearnerGoal> goals = const [];
  bool isBusy = false;

  Future<void> load() async {
    isBusy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      goals = await _repository.load(learnerId);
      errorMessage = null;
    } catch (_) {
      errorMessage = 'Goals could not be loaded.';
    } finally {
      isBusy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> save(LearnerGoal goal) async {
    if (goal.learnerId != learnerId || goal.title.trim().isEmpty) {
      errorMessage = 'Enter a goal title.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    isBusy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.save(goal);
      goals = await _repository.load(learnerId);
      errorMessage = null;
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      errorMessage = 'The goal could not be saved.';
      return false;
    } finally {
      isBusy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> delete(String goalId) async {
    await _repository.delete(goalId: goalId, learnerId: learnerId);
    goals = await _repository.load(learnerId);
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

import '../../../../core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/learner_goal.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/goals_repository.dart';

final class SqliteGoalsRepository implements GoalsRepository {
  const SqliteGoalsRepository(this._database);
  final LocalDatabase _database;

  @override
  Future<void> delete({
    required String goalId,
    required String learnerId,
  }) async {
    _database.raw.execute(
      'DELETE FROM learner_goals WHERE id = ? AND learner_id = ?',
      [goalId, learnerId],
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<List<LearnerGoal>> load(String learnerId) async {
    final rows = _database.raw.select(
      '''
      SELECT * FROM learner_goals
      WHERE learner_id = ?
      ORDER BY CASE status WHEN 'active' THEN 0 WHEN 'completed' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END,
               priority ASC, updated_at DESC
    ''',
      [learnerId],
    );
    return rows
        .map(
          (row) => LearnerGoal(
            createdAt: DateTime.parse(row['created_at'] as String),
            description: row['description'] as String,
            id: row['id'] as String,
            learnerId: row['learner_id'] as String,
            occupationalContext: row['occupational_context'] as String,
            priority: row['priority'] as int,
            status: GoalStatus.values.byName(row['status'] as String),
            targetDate: row['target_date'] == null
                ? null
                : DateTime.parse(row['target_date'] as String),
            title: row['title'] as String,
            updatedAt: DateTime.parse(row['updated_at'] as String),
          ),
        )
        .toList(growable: false);
  }

  @override
  Future<void> save(LearnerGoal goal) async {
    _database.raw.execute(
      '''
      INSERT INTO learner_goals (
        id, learner_id, title, description, occupational_context,
        priority, status, target_date, created_at, updated_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET
        title = excluded.title,
        description = excluded.description,
        occupational_context = excluded.occupational_context,
        priority = excluded.priority,
        status = excluded.status,
        target_date = excluded.target_date,
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at
      WHERE learner_goals.learner_id = excluded.learner_id;
    ''',
      [
        goal.id,
        goal.learnerId,
        goal.title,
        goal.description,
        goal.occupationalContext,
        goal.priority,
        goal.status.name,
        goal.targetDate?.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
        goal.createdAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
        goal.updatedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

enum GoalStatus { active, archived, completed }

final class LearnerGoal {
  const LearnerGoal({
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.description,
    required this.id,
    required this.learnerId,
    required this.occupationalContext,
    required this.priority,
    required this.status,
    required this.title,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.targetDate,
  });

  final DateTime createdAt;
  final String description;
  final String id;
  final String learnerId;
  final String occupationalContext;
  final int priority;
  final GoalStatus status;
  final DateTime? targetDate;
  final String title;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../../app/phase2_providers.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/learner_goal.dart';

final class GoalEditorPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const GoalEditorPage({this.goalId, super.key});
  final String? goalId;
  @override
  ConsumerState<GoalEditorPage> createState() => _State();
}

final class _State extends ConsumerState<GoalEditorPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _title = TextEditingController();
  final _description = TextEditingController();
  final _context = TextEditingController(text: 'Procurement communication');
  int _priority = 1;
  GoalStatus _status = GoalStatus.active;
  bool _seeded = false;
  @override
  void dispose() {
    _title.dispose();
    _description.dispose();
    _context.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final state = ref.watch(goalsControllerProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(widget.goalId == null ? 'Create goal' : 'Edit goal'),
      ),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (e, s) => Center(child: Text('$e')),
        data: (controller) {
          LearnerGoal? existing;
          if (widget.goalId != null) {
            for (final g in controller.goals) {
              if (g.id == widget.goalId) existing = g;
            }
          }
          if (!_seeded && existing != null) {
            _title.text = existing.title;
            _description.text = existing.description;
            _context.text = existing.occupationalContext;
            _priority = existing.priority;
            _status = existing.status;
            _seeded = true;
          }
          return Center(
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 700),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: ListView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  children: [
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _title,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Goal title',
                      ),
                      validator: (v) =>
                          v == null || v.trim().isEmpty ? 'Required' : null,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _description,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Description',
                      ),
                      minLines: 2,
                      maxLines: 4,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _context,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Occupational context',
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    DropdownButtonFormField<int>(
                      initialValue: _priority,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Priority'),
                      items: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]
                          .map(
                            (e) =>
                                DropdownMenuItem(value: e, child: Text('$e')),
                          )
                          .toList(),
                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _priority = v!),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    DropdownButtonFormField<GoalStatus>(
                      initialValue: _status,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Status'),
                      items: GoalStatus.values
                          .map(
                            (e) =>
                                DropdownMenuItem(value: e, child: Text(e.name)),
                          )
                          .toList(),
                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _status = v!),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    FilledButton(
                      onPressed: controller.isBusy
                          ? null
                          : () => _save(controller, existing),
                      child: Text(
                        controller.isBusy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save goal',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  Future<void> _save(dynamic controller, LearnerGoal? existing) async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    final now = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    final goal = LearnerGoal(
      createdAt: existing?.createdAt ?? now,
      description: _description.text.trim(),
      id:
          existing?.id ??
          '${controller.learnerId}-${now.microsecondsSinceEpoch}',
      learnerId: controller.learnerId,
      occupationalContext: _context.text.trim(),
      priority: _priority,
      status: _status,
      title: _title.text.trim(),
      updatedAt: now,
    );
    if (await controller.save(goal) && mounted) context.go('/goals');
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../../app/phase2_providers.dart';

final class GoalsPage extends ConsumerWidget {
  const GoalsPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final state = ref.watch(goalsControllerProvider);

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Learning goals'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () => context.go('/home'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.home),
            tooltip: 'Home',
          ),
        ],
      ),
      floatingActionButton: FloatingActionButton.extended(
        onPressed: () => context.go('/goals/edit'),
        icon: const Icon(Icons.add),
        label: const Text('New goal'),
      ),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (error, stackTrace) =>
            Center(child: Text('Goals could not be loaded: $error')),
        data: (controller) => ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: controller,
          builder: (context, child) {
            if (controller.goals.isEmpty) {
              return const Center(
                child: Text(
                  'No goals yet. Create the first occupational English goal.',
                ),
              );
            }

            return ListView.separated(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              itemCount: controller.goals.length,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(height: 8),
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final goal = controller.goals[index];
                return Card(
                  child: ListTile(
                    leading: CircleAvatar(child: Text('${goal.priority}')),
                    title: Text(goal.title),
                    subtitle: Text(
                      '${goal.occupationalContext}\n${goal.status.name}',
                    ),
                    isThreeLine: true,
                    trailing: PopupMenuButton<String>(
                      onSelected: (value) {
                        if (value == 'edit') {
                          context.go('/goals/edit?id=${goal.id}');
                        }
                        if (value == 'delete') {
                          controller.delete(goal.id);
                        }
                      },
                      itemBuilder: (context) => const [
                        PopupMenuItem(value: 'edit', child: Text('Edit')),
                        PopupMenuItem(value: 'delete', child: Text('Delete')),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

Future<void> _switchProfile(
  WidgetRef ref,
  AuthenticationController authentication,
) async {
  ref.invalidate(learnerProfileControllerProvider);
  ref.invalidate(goalsControllerProvider);
  ref.invalidate(learningSessionControllerProvider);
  await authentication.signOut();
}

final class _SetupStep extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SetupStep({
    required this.action,
    required this.detail,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.title,
  });
  final String action;
  final String detail;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final String title;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Row(
    children: [
      Icon(icon, size: 28),
      const SizedBox(width: 14),
      Expanded(
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall),
            const SizedBox(height: 3),
            Text(detail),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      TextButton(onPressed: onTap, child: Text(action)),
    ],
  );
}

final class _SummaryCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SummaryCard({
    required this.actionLabel,
    required this.body,
    required this.icon,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.title,
    this.emphasized = false,
  });

  final String actionLabel;
  final String body;
  final bool emphasized;
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final String title;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colorScheme = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Semantics(
      button: true,
      label: '$title. $body. $actionLabel',
      child: SizedBox(
        width: 300,
        child: Card(
          color: emphasized ? colorScheme.primaryContainer : null,
          clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
          child: InkWell(
            onTap: onTap,
            mouseCursor: SystemMouseCursors.click,
            child: Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Icon(
                    icon,
                    size: 32,
                    color: emphasized ? colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer : null,
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(height: 12),
                  Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium),
                  const SizedBox(height: 8),
                  Text(body),
                  const SizedBox(height: 16),
                  Row(
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          actionLabel,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: emphasized
                                ? colorScheme.onPrimaryContainer
                                : colorScheme.primary,
                            fontWeight: FontWeight.w600,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const Icon(Icons.arrow_forward),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';

import '../../domain/entities/learner_profile.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/learner_profile_repository.dart';

final class LearnerProfileController extends ChangeNotifier {
  LearnerProfileController(this._repository, this.learnerId);

  final LearnerProfileRepository _repository;
  final String learnerId;

  String? errorMessage;
  bool isBusy = false;
  LearnerProfile? profile;

  Future<void> load() async {
    isBusy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      profile = await _repository.load(learnerId);
      errorMessage = null;
    } catch (_) {
      errorMessage = 'The learner profile could not be loaded.';
    } finally {
      isBusy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<bool> save(LearnerProfile value) async {
    if (!value.isComplete || value.preferredSessionMinutes < 5) {
      errorMessage = 'Complete the required profile fields.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    isBusy = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      await _repository.save(value);
      profile = value;
      errorMessage = null;
      return true;
    } catch (_) {
      errorMessage = 'The learner profile could not be saved.';
      return false;
    } finally {
      isBusy = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }
}

import '../../../../core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import '../../domain/entities/learner_profile.dart';
import '../../domain/repositories/learner_profile_repository.dart';

final class SqliteLearnerProfileRepository implements LearnerProfileRepository {
  const SqliteLearnerProfileRepository(this._database);
  final LocalDatabase _database;

  @override
  Future<LearnerProfile?> load(String learnerId) async {
    final rows = _database.raw.select(
      'SELECT * FROM learner_profiles WHERE learner_id = ?',
      [learnerId],
    );
    if (rows.isEmpty) return null;
    final row = rows.first;
    return LearnerProfile(
      accessibilityNotes: row['accessibility_notes'] as String,
      communicationTasks: row['communication_tasks'] as String,
      displayName: row['display_name'] as String,
      englishLevel: row['english_level'] as String,
      industry: row['industry'] as String,
      learnerId: row['learner_id'] as String,
      nativeLanguage: row['native_language'] as String,
      occupationalRole: row['occupational_role'] as String,
      preferredSessionMinutes: row['preferred_session_minutes'] as int,
      procurementExperienceYears: row['procurement_experience_years'] as int,
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(row['updated_at'] as String),
    );
  }

  @override
  Future<void> save(LearnerProfile profile) async {
    _database.raw.execute(
      '''
      INSERT INTO learner_profiles (
        learner_id, display_name, native_language, english_level,
        occupational_role, industry, procurement_experience_years,
        communication_tasks, preferred_session_minutes,
        accessibility_notes, updated_at
      ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
      ON CONFLICT(learner_id) DO UPDATE SET
        display_name = excluded.display_name,
        native_language = excluded.native_language,
        english_level = excluded.english_level,
        occupational_role = excluded.occupational_role,
        industry = excluded.industry,
        procurement_experience_years = excluded.procurement_experience_years,
        communication_tasks = excluded.communication_tasks,
        preferred_session_minutes = excluded.preferred_session_minutes,
        accessibility_notes = excluded.accessibility_notes,
        updated_at = excluded.updated_at;
    ''',
      [
        profile.learnerId,
        profile.displayName,
        profile.nativeLanguage,
        profile.englishLevel,
        profile.occupationalRole,
        profile.industry,
        profile.procurementExperienceYears,
        profile.communicationTasks,
        profile.preferredSessionMinutes,
        profile.accessibilityNotes,
        profile.updatedAt.toUtc().toIso8601String(),
      ],
    );
  }
}

final class LearnerProfile {
  const LearnerProfile({
    required this.accessibilityNotes,
    required this.communicationTasks,
    required this.displayName,
    required this.englishLevel,
    required this.industry,
    required this.learnerId,
    required this.nativeLanguage,
    required this.occupationalRole,
    required this.preferredSessionMinutes,
    required this.procurementExperienceYears,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  final String accessibilityNotes;
  final String communicationTasks;
  final String displayName;
  final String englishLevel;
  final String industry;
  final String learnerId;
  final String nativeLanguage;
  final String occupationalRole;
  final int preferredSessionMinutes;
  final int procurementExperienceYears;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  bool get isComplete =>
      displayName.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      nativeLanguage.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      englishLevel.trim().isNotEmpty &&
      occupationalRole.trim().isNotEmpty;
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';

import '../../../../app/phase2_providers.dart';

final class LearnerProfileEditPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const LearnerProfileEditPage({super.key});
  @override
  ConsumerState<LearnerProfileEditPage> createState() => _State();
}

final class _State extends ConsumerState<LearnerProfileEditPage> {
  final _formKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  final _displayName = TextEditingController();
  final _nativeLanguage = TextEditingController(text: 'Turkish');
  final _role = TextEditingController(text: 'Procurement');
  final _industry = TextEditingController(text: 'Automotive');
  final _experience = TextEditingController(text: '0');
  final _tasks = TextEditingController(
    text: 'Supplier meetings, RFQ clarification, delivery escalation',
  );
  final _accessibility = TextEditingController();
  String _level = 'B1';
  double _minutes = 25;
  bool _seeded = false;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    for (final value in [
      _displayName,
      _nativeLanguage,
      _role,
      _industry,
      _experience,
      _tasks,
      _accessibility,
    ]) {
      value.dispose();
    }
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final state = ref.watch(learnerProfileControllerProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('Edit learner profile')),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (error, stack) => Center(child: Text('$error')),
        data: (controller) {
          if (!_seeded) {
            final p = controller.profile;
            if (p != null) {
              _displayName.text = p.displayName;
              _nativeLanguage.text = p.nativeLanguage;
              _role.text = p.occupationalRole;
              _industry.text = p.industry;
              _experience.text = '${p.procurementExperienceYears}';
              _tasks.text = p.communicationTasks;
              _accessibility.text = p.accessibilityNotes;
              _level = p.englishLevel;
              _minutes = p.preferredSessionMinutes.toDouble();
            }
            _seeded = true;
          }
          return Center(
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 760),
              child: Form(
                key: _formKey,
                child: ListView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  children: [
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _displayName,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Display name',
                      ),
                      validator: _required,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _nativeLanguage,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Native language',
                      ),
                      validator: _required,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    DropdownButtonFormField<String>(
                      initialValue: _level,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Current English level',
                      ),
                      items: ['A1', 'A2', 'B1', 'B2', 'C1', 'C2']
                          .map(
                            (e) => DropdownMenuItem(value: e, child: Text(e)),
                          )
                          .toList(),
                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _level = v!),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _role,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Occupational role',
                      ),
                      validator: _required,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _industry,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(labelText: 'Industry'),
                      validator: _required,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _experience,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Procurement experience (years)',
                      ),
                      keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _tasks,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Recurring English communication tasks',
                      ),
                      minLines: 2,
                      maxLines: 4,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Text('Preferred session: ${_minutes.round()} minutes'),
                    Slider(
                      value: _minutes,
                      min: 10,
                      max: 60,
                      divisions: 10,
                      label: '${_minutes.round()}',
                      onChanged: (v) => setState(() => _minutes = v),
                    ),
                    TextFormField(
                      controller: _accessibility,
                      decoration: const InputDecoration(
                        labelText: 'Accessibility preferences or notes',
                      ),
                      minLines: 2,
                      maxLines: 3,
                    ),
                    if (controller.errorMessage != null)
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                        child: Text(
                          controller.errorMessage!,
                          style: TextStyle(
                            color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.error,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 20),
                    FilledButton(
                      onPressed: controller.isBusy
                          ? null
                          : () => _save(controller),
                      child: Text(
                        controller.isBusy ? 'Saving...' : 'Save profile',
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          );
        },
      ),
    );
  }

  String? _required(String? value) =>
      value == null || value.trim().isEmpty ? 'Required' : null;
  Future<void> _save(dynamic controller) async {
    if (!_formKey.currentState!.validate()) return;
    final value = LearnerProfile(
      accessibilityNotes: _accessibility.text.trim(),
      communicationTasks: _tasks.text.trim(),
      displayName: _displayName.text.trim(),
      englishLevel: _level,
      industry: _industry.text.trim(),
      learnerId: controller.learnerId,
      nativeLanguage: _nativeLanguage.text.trim(),
      occupationalRole: _role.text.trim(),
      preferredSessionMinutes: _minutes.round(),
      procurementExperienceYears: int.tryParse(_experience.text) ?? 0,
      updatedAt: DateTime.now().toUtc(),
    );
    if (await controller.save(value) && mounted) context.go('/profile');
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import '../../ai_coach/ai_coach.dart';
import '../curriculum.dart';
import '../learning_session.dart';

final class LearningSessionController extends ChangeNotifier {
  LearningSessionController(this.repo, this.learnerId);
  final LearningSessionRepository repo;
  final String learnerId;
  final coach = const OfflineProcurementAiCoach();
  LearningSession? session;
  List<LearningSession> history = [];
  List<CurriculumActivity> queue = [];
  CoachFeedback? feedback;
  String? errorMessage;
  CurriculumActivity? get current =>
      session == null || session!.currentStep >= queue.length
      ? null
      : queue[session!.currentStep];
  List<MasteryRecord> get mastery => repo.mastery(learnerId);
  double get overallMastery => mastery.isEmpty
      ? 0
      : mastery.map((e) => e.mastery).reduce((a, b) => a + b) / mastery.length;
  void load() {
    history = repo.history(learnerId);
    session = repo.active(learnerId);
    if (session != null) {
      final ids = repo.loadQueue(learnerId, session!.id);
      queue = ids
          .map(
            (id) =>
                ProcurementCurriculum.activities.firstWhere((a) => a.id == id),
          )
          .toList();
    }
    if (queue.isEmpty) _build();
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void _build() {
    final scores = {for (final m in mastery) m.activityId: m.mastery},
        items = [...ProcurementCurriculum.activities]
          ..sort((a, b) => (scores[a.id] ?? 0).compareTo(scores[b.id] ?? 0));
    queue = items.take(8).toList();
  }

  void start() {
    _build();
    final n = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    session = LearningSession(
      id: '$learnerId-${n.microsecondsSinceEpoch}',
      learnerId: learnerId,
      status: LearningSessionStatus.active,
      currentStep: 0,
      totalSteps: queue.length,
      startedAt: n,
      updatedAt: n,
    );
    repo.save(session!);
    repo.saveQueue(learnerId, session!.id, queue);
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool check(String answer) {
    final a = current;
    if (a == null || answer.trim().isEmpty) {
      errorMessage = 'Write or select an answer.';
      notifyListeners();
      return false;
    }
    feedback = coach.evaluate(a, answer);
    repo.record(learnerId, a, feedback!.score);
    errorMessage = null;
    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }

  void next() {
    final s = session;
    if (s == null || feedback == null) return;
    final step = s.currentStep + 1, n = DateTime.now().toUtc();
    session = LearningSession(
      id: s.id,
      learnerId: s.learnerId,
      status: step >= queue.length
          ? LearningSessionStatus.completed
          : LearningSessionStatus.active,
      currentStep: step,
      totalSteps: queue.length,
      startedAt: s.startedAt,
      completedAt: step >= queue.length ? n : null,
      updatedAt: n,
    );
    repo.save(session!);
    feedback = null;
    history = repo.history(learnerId);
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

enum LearningSkill {
  vocabulary,
  reading,
  grammar,
  writing,
  negotiation,
  meeting,
}

enum ActivityKind { recall, choice, reading, rewrite, writing, simulation }

final class CurriculumActivity {
  const CurriculumActivity(
    this.id,
    this.title,
    this.skill,
    this.kind,
    this.prompt,
    this.answer,
    this.translation,
    this.explanation,
    this.model, {
    this.options = const [],
  });
  final String id, title, prompt, answer, translation, explanation, model;
  final LearningSkill skill;
  final ActivityKind kind;
  final List<String> options;
}
