import '../../core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'curriculum.dart';

enum LearningSessionStatus { active, completed }

final class LearningSession {
  const LearningSession({
    required this.id,
    required this.learnerId,
    required this.status,
    required this.currentStep,
    required this.totalSteps,
    required this.startedAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.completedAt,
  });
  final String id, learnerId;
  final LearningSessionStatus status;
  final int currentStep, totalSteps;
  final DateTime startedAt, updatedAt;
  final DateTime? completedAt;
}

final class MasteryRecord {
  const MasteryRecord(this.activityId, this.skill, this.mastery, this.attempts);
  final String activityId, skill;
  final double mastery;
  final int attempts;
}

final class LearningSessionRepository {
  const LearningSessionRepository(this.db);
  final LocalDatabase db;
  LearningSession? active(String l) {
    final r = db.raw.select(
      "SELECT * FROM learning_sessions WHERE learner_id=? AND status='active' ORDER BY updated_at DESC LIMIT 1",
      [l],
    );
    return r.isEmpty ? null : _m(r.first);
  }

  List<LearningSession> history(String l) => db.raw
      .select(
        "SELECT * FROM learning_sessions WHERE learner_id=? AND status='completed'",
        [l],
      )
      .map(_m)
      .toList();
  void save(LearningSession s) => db.raw.execute(
    "INSERT INTO learning_sessions VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(id) DO UPDATE SET status=excluded.status,current_step=excluded.current_step,total_steps=excluded.total_steps,completed_at=excluded.completed_at,updated_at=excluded.updated_at",
    [
      s.id,
      s.learnerId,
      'Adaptive procurement English',
      s.status.name,
      s.currentStep,
      s.totalSteps,
      s.startedAt.toIso8601String(),
      s.completedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      s.updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
    ],
  );
  void saveQueue(String l, String id, List<CurriculumActivity> q) {
    db.raw.execute(
      'DELETE FROM session_activities WHERE learner_id=? AND session_id=?',
      [l, id],
    );
    for (var i = 0; i < q.length; i++) {
      db.raw.execute('INSERT INTO session_activities VALUES(?,?,?,?)', [
        id,
        l,
        i,
        q[i].id,
      ]);
    }
  }

  List<String> loadQueue(String l, String id) => db.raw
      .select(
        'SELECT activity_key FROM session_activities WHERE learner_id=? AND session_id=? ORDER BY position',
        [l, id],
      )
      .map((r) => r['activity_key'] as String)
      .toList();
  List<MasteryRecord> mastery(String l) => db.raw
      .select('SELECT * FROM activity_mastery WHERE learner_id=?', [l])
      .map(
        (r) => MasteryRecord(
          r['activity_key'] as String,
          r['skill'] as String,
          r['mastery'] as double,
          r['attempts'] as int,
        ),
      )
      .toList();
  void record(String l, CurriculumActivity a, int score) {
    final n = DateTime.now().toUtc(),
        old = db.raw.select(
          'SELECT attempts,mastery FROM activity_mastery WHERE learner_id=? AND activity_key=?',
          [l, a.id],
        ),
        attempts = old.isEmpty ? 1 : (old.first['attempts'] as int) + 1,
        previous = old.isEmpty ? 0.0 : old.first['mastery'] as double,
        m = (previous * .65 + score / 100 * .35).clamp(0.0, 1.0);
    db.raw.execute(
      'INSERT INTO activity_mastery VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?) ON CONFLICT(learner_id,activity_key) DO UPDATE SET attempts=excluded.attempts,mastery=excluded.mastery,next_review_at=excluded.next_review_at',
      [
        l,
        a.id,
        a.skill.name,
        attempts,
        m,
        n
            .add(
              Duration(
                days: m >= .8
                    ? 7
                    : m >= .6
                    ? 3
                    : 1,
              ),
            )
            .toIso8601String(),
      ],
    );
  }

  LearningSession _m(dynamic r) => LearningSession(
    id: r['id'] as String,
    learnerId: r['learner_id'] as String,
    status: LearningSessionStatus.values.byName(r['status'] as String),
    currentStep: r['current_step'] as int,
    totalSteps: r['total_steps'] as int,
    startedAt: DateTime.parse(r['started_at'] as String),
    completedAt: r['completed_at'] == null
        ? null
        : DateTime.parse(r['completed_at'] as String),
    updatedAt: DateTime.parse(r['updated_at'] as String),
  );
}

final class LearningInsightsPage extends ConsumerWidget {
  const LearningInsightsPage({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final state = ref.watch(learningSessionControllerProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Learning insights'),
        actions: [
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => context.go('/home'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.home),
            label: const Text('Today'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
        ],
      ),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (error, stack) =>
            Center(child: Text('Insights could not be loaded: $error')),
        data: (controller) => ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: controller,
          builder: (context, child) {
            final mastery = (controller.overallMastery * 100).round();
            final completed = controller.history
                .where(
                  (session) =>
                      session.status == LearningSessionStatus.completed,
                )
                .length;
            final weak = [...controller.mastery]
              ..sort((a, b) => a.mastery.compareTo(b.mastery));
            return Center(
              child: ConstrainedBox(
                constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 1060),
                child: ListView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      'Private learning evidence',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,

                    const Text(
                      'All metrics belong only to the currently unlocked local profile.',
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 24),
                    Wrap(
                      spacing: 16,
                      runSpacing: 16,
                      children: [
                        _MetricCard(
                          icon: Icons.insights,
                          label: 'Overall mastery',
                          value: '$mastery%',
                        ),
                        _MetricCard(
                          icon: Icons.task_alt,
                          label: 'Completed sessions',
                          value: '$completed',
                        ),
                        _MetricCard(
                          icon: Icons.school,
                          label: 'Curriculum activities',
                          value: '${ProcurementCurriculum.activities.length}',
                        ),
                        _MetricCard(
                          icon: Icons.playlist_play,
                          label: 'Today queue',
                          value: '${controller.queue.length}',
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 28),
                    Card(
                      color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                        child: Row(
                          children: [
                            const Icon(Icons.bolt, size: 34),
                            const SizedBox(width: 16),
                            Expanded(
                              child: Column(
                                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                children: [
                                  Text(
                                    controller.session == null
                                        ? 'Start today’s focused session'
                                        : 'Continue today’s active session',
                                    style: Theme.of(
                                      context,
                                    ).textTheme.titleMedium,
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                  Text(
                                    controller.current == null
                                        ? 'The adaptive planner will prioritize lower-mastery activities.'
                                        : 'Next: ${controller.current!.title}',
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ),
                            FilledButton.icon(
                              onPressed: () => context.go(
                                controller.session == null
                                    ? '/learning-plan'
                                    : '/session',
                              ),
                              icon: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow),
                              label: const Text('Continue learning'),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 28),
                    Text(
                      'Priority review',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    if (weak.isEmpty)
                      const Card(
                        child: ListTile(
                          leading: Icon(Icons.info_outline),
                          title: Text(
                            'Complete activities to build mastery evidence.',
                          ),
                          subtitle: Text(
                            'The weakest skills will appear here automatically.',
                          ),
                        ),
                      )
                    else
                      for (final item in weak.take(5))
                        Card(

                            leading: CircleAvatar(
                              child: Text('${(item.mastery * 100).round()}%'),

                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 16),
                    OutlinedButton.icon(
                      onPressed: () => context.go('/progress'),
                      icon: const Icon(Icons.bar_chart),
                      label: const Text('Open detailed skill progress'),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

final class _MetricCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _MetricCard({
    required this.icon,
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
  });

              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,

                Text(value, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall),
                Text(label),
              ],
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    ),
  );
}

                icon: const Icon(Icons.play_arrow),
                label: Text(
                  c.session?.status == LearningSessionStatus.active
                      ? 'Resume session'
                      : 'Start adaptive session',
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import '../../../app/phase2_providers.dart';
import '../curriculum.dart';
import '../learning_session.dart';

final class LearningSessionPage extends ConsumerStatefulWidget {
  const LearningSessionPage({super.key});
  @override
  ConsumerState<LearningSessionPage> createState() => _S();
}

final class _S extends ConsumerState<LearningSessionPage> {
  final answer = TextEditingController();
  @override
  void dispose() {
    answer.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final state = ref.watch(learningSessionControllerProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(title: const Text('AI-guided practice')),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (e, s) => Center(child: Text('$e')),
        data: (c) => ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: c,
          builder: (context, child) {
            final a = c.current, s = c.session;
            if (s == null) {
              return Center(
                child: FilledButton(
                  onPressed: c.start,
                  child: const Text('Start'),
                ),
              );
            }
            if (a == null || s.status == LearningSessionStatus.completed) {
              return Center(
                child: FilledButton(
                  onPressed: () => context.go('/progress'),
                  child: const Text('View progress'),
                ),
              );
            }
            return ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(28),
              children: [
                LinearProgressIndicator(value: s.currentStep / s.totalSteps),
                Text(
                  'Activity ${s.currentStep + 1} of ${s.totalSteps} • ${a.skill.name}',
                ),
                Card(
                  color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.secondaryContainer,
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(
                          a.title,
                          style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleLarge,
                        ),
                        Text(a.explanation),
                        Text('Türkçe: ${a.translation}'),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Card(
                  child: Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                    child: Text(a.prompt),
                  ),
                ),
                if (a.options.isNotEmpty)
                  Wrap(
                    children: [
                      for (final o in a.options)
                        ChoiceChip(
                          label: Text(o),
                          selected: answer.text == o,
                          onSelected: c.feedback == null
                              ? (v) {
                                  if (v) {
                                    setState(() => answer.text = o);
                                  }
                                }
                              : null,
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                if (a.options.isEmpty)
                  TextField(
                    controller: answer,
                    enabled: c.feedback == null,
                    minLines:
                        a.kind == ActivityKind.writing ||
                            a.kind == ActivityKind.simulation
                        ? 4
                        : 1,
                    maxLines: 7,
                    decoration: const InputDecoration(
                      labelText: 'Your English response',
                    ),
                  ),
                if (c.errorMessage != null) Text(c.errorMessage!),
                if (c.feedback == null)
                  FilledButton.icon(
                    onPressed: () => c.check(answer.text),
                    icon: const Icon(Icons.auto_awesome),
                    label: const Text('Ask AI Coach'),
                  ),
                if (c.feedback != null)
                  Card(
                    color: Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                    child: Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text('AI Coach • ${c.feedback!.score}/100'),
                          Text(c.feedback!.message),
                          for (final x in c.feedback!.strengths) Text('✓ $x'),
                          for (final x in c.feedback!.targets) Text('• $x'),
                          Text('Model: ${c.feedback!.model}'),
                          FilledButton(
                            onPressed: () {
                              answer.clear();
                              c.next();
                            },
                            child: const Text('Continue'),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
              ],
            );
          },
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:flutter_riverpod/flutter_riverpod.dart';
import 'package:go_router/go_router.dart';
import '../../../app/phase2_providers.dart';
import '../curriculum.dart';
import '../learning_session.dart';

final class ProgressPage extends ConsumerWidget {
  const ProgressPage({super.key});
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context, WidgetRef ref) {
    final state = ref.watch(learningSessionControllerProvider);
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Progress'),
        actions: [
          IconButton(
            onPressed: () => context.go('/home'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.home),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      body: state.when(
        loading: () => const Center(child: CircularProgressIndicator()),
        error: (e, s) => Center(child: Text('$e')),
        data: (c) => ListenableBuilder(
          listenable: c,
          builder: (context, child) => ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(28),
            children: [
              Text(
                'Overall mastery ${(c.overallMastery * 100).round()}%',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
              ),
              for (final skill in LearningSkill.values)
                Card(
                  child: ListTile(
                    title: Text(skill.name),
                    subtitle: LinearProgressIndicator(
                      value: _v(c.mastery, skill.name),
                    ),
                    trailing: Text(
                      '${(_v(c.mastery, skill.name) * 100).round()}%',
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  double _v(List<MasteryRecord> r, String s) {
    final v = r.where((x) => x.skill == s).map((x) => x.mastery).toList();
    return v.isEmpty ? 0 : v.reduce((a, b) => a + b) / v.length;
  }
}

  final int minutes;
  final String context;

  final List<String> keyPhrases;
}

        'You have five minutes. Please explain the recommendation, the risk, and the decision required.',

final class SimulationLabPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SimulationLabPage({super.key});

  @override
  State<SimulationLabPage> createState() => _SimulationLabPageState();
}

final class _SimulationLabPageState extends State<SimulationLabPage> {
  String filter = 'All';

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final categories = <String>[
      'All',
      ...{for (final scenario in simulationScenarios) scenario.category},
    ];
    final visible = filter == 'All'
        ? simulationScenarios
        : simulationScenarios
              .where((scenario) => scenario.category == filter)
              .toList();

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: const Text('Real-world simulation lab'),
        actions: <Widget>[
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => context.go('/home'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.home),
            label: const Text('Today'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
        ],
      ),
      body: Center(
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 1120),
          child: ListView(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(32),
            children: <Widget>[
              Text(
                'Practice English under realistic pressure',
                style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 8),
              const Text(
                'General English, workplace communication, procurement, manufacturing, meetings, calls, email, presentations, and travel. Each simulation includes roles, context, objectives, changing pressure, and structured feedback.',
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 20),
              Wrap(
                spacing: 8,
                runSpacing: 8,
                children: <Widget>[
                  for (final category in categories)
                    ChoiceChip(
                      label: Text(category),
                      selected: filter == category,
                      onSelected: (_) => setState(() => filter = category),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: 24),
              GridView.builder(
                shrinkWrap: true,
                physics: const NeverScrollableScrollPhysics(),
                gridDelegate: const SliverGridDelegateWithMaxCrossAxisExtent(
                  maxCrossAxisExtent: 355,
                  mainAxisExtent: 260,
                  crossAxisSpacing: 16,
                  mainAxisSpacing: 16,
                ),
                itemCount: visible.length,
                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                  final scenario = visible[index];
                  return Card(
                    clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
                    child: InkWell(
                      onTap: () => context.go('/simulation/${scenario.id}'),
                      child: Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20),
                        child: Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: <Widget>[
                            Row(
                              children: <Widget>[
                                CircleAvatar(child: Icon(_icon(scenario.id))),
                                const Spacer(),
                                Chip(label: Text(scenario.level)),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            Text(
                              scenario.title,
                              style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 8),
                            Text(
                              scenario.context,
                              maxLines: 3,
                              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                            ),
                            const Spacer(),
                            Row(
                              children: <Widget>[
                                const Icon(Icons.schedule, size: 18),
                                const SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Text('${scenario.minutes} min'),
                                const Spacer(),
                                Text(
                                  scenario.category,
                                  style: Theme.of(
                                    context,
                                  ).textTheme.labelMedium,
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                            const SizedBox(height: 12),
                            const Row(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
                              children: <Widget>[
                                Text('Open scenario'),
                                SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Icon(Icons.arrow_forward),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  );
                },
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  IconData _icon(String id) {
    if (id.contains('factory')) {
      return Icons.precision_manufacturing;
    }
    if (id.contains('email')) {
      return Icons.email;
    }
    if (id.contains('teams')) {
      return Icons.groups;
    }
    if (id.contains('travel')) {
      return Icons.flight;
    }
    if (id.contains('phone')) {
      return Icons.call;
    }
    if (id.contains('presentation')) {
      return Icons.present_to_all;
    }
    return Icons.record_voice_over;
  }
}

final class SimulationRoomPage extends StatefulWidget {
  const SimulationRoomPage({required this.id, super.key});

  final String id;

  @override
  State<SimulationRoomPage> createState() => _SimulationRoomPageState();
}

final class _SimulationRoomPageState extends State<SimulationRoomPage> {
  final TextEditingController answer = TextEditingController();
  final List<String> transcript = <String>[];
  int turn = 0;
  String? feedback;

  SimulationScenario get scenario => simulationScenarios.firstWhere(
    (item) => item.id == widget.id,
    orElse: () => simulationScenarios.first,
  );

  @override
  void dispose() {
    answer.dispose();
    super.dispose();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final currentScenario = scenario;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        title: Text(currentScenario.title),
        actions: <Widget>[
          TextButton.icon(
            onPressed: () => context.go('/simulations'),
            icon: const Icon(Icons.grid_view),
            label: const Text('All simulations'),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 12),
        ],
      ),
      body: Center(
        child: ConstrainedBox(
          constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 1120),
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
            children: <Widget>[
              Expanded(
                flex: 4,
                child: ListView(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  children: <Widget>[
                    Text(
                      'Briefing',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _Info('Context', currentScenario.context),
                    _Info('Your objective', currentScenario.objective),
                    _Info('Pressure', currentScenario.pressure),
                    _Info('Roles', currentScenario.roles.join('\n')),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    Text(
                      'Useful language',
                      style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleMedium,
                    ),
                    for (final phrase in currentScenario.keyPhrases)
                      ListTile(
                        dense: true,
                        leading: const Icon(Icons.chat_bubble_outline),
                        title: Text(phrase),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const VerticalDivider(width: 1),
              Expanded(
                flex: 6,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: <Widget>[
                      Text(
                        'Live role-play',
                        style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.headlineSmall,
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Text(
                        'Turn ${turn + 1} • Respond naturally. The scenario develops from your answer.',
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Expanded(
                        child: Card(
                          color: Theme.of(
                            context,
                          ).colorScheme.surfaceContainerHighest,
                          child: ListView(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(18),
                            children: <Widget>[
                              _Bubble(
                                'Scenario partner',
                                turn == 0
                                    ? currentScenario.opening
                                    : _promptFor(turn),
                                false,
                              ),
                              for (
                                var index = 0;
                                index < transcript.length;
                                index++
                              )
                                _Bubble(
                                  index.isEven ? 'You' : 'Scenario partner',
                                  transcript[index],
                                  index.isEven,
                                ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 12),
                      TextField(
                        controller: answer,
                        minLines: 2,
                        maxLines: 5,
                        decoration: const InputDecoration(
                          labelText: 'Your English response',
                          hintText:
                              'Write what you would say or send in this situation…',
                        ),
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      Row(
                        children: <Widget>[
                          OutlinedButton.icon(
                            onPressed: _hint,
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.lightbulb_outline),
                            label: const Text('Coaching hint'),
                          ),
                          const Spacer(),
                          FilledButton.icon(
                            onPressed: _submit,
                            icon: const Icon(Icons.send),
                            label: Text(
                              turn >= 3 ? 'Finish and review' : 'Send response',
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      if (feedback != null)
                        Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 12),
                          child: Card(
                            color: Theme.of(
                              context,
                            ).colorScheme.primaryContainer,
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(16),
                              child: Text(feedback!),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  void _submit() {
    final text = answer.text.trim();
    if (text.isEmpty) {
      return;
    }
    setState(() {
      transcript.add(text);
      answer.clear();
      final score = _score(text);
      transcript.add(_reply());
      turn++;
      feedback = 'Coach snapshot: $score/100 • ${_feedback(text)}';
      if (turn >= 4) {
        feedback =
            'Simulation review: $score/100 • You completed $turn response turns. ${_feedback(text)} Repeat the scenario using one clarification question, one evidence-based statement, and one explicit next action.';
      }
    });
  }

  int _score(String text) {
    var score = 45;
    final wordCount = text.split(RegExp(r'\s+')).length;
    if (wordCount >= 12) {
      score += 15;
    }
    if (text.contains('?')) {
      score += 10;
    }
    if (RegExp(
      r'\b(please|could|would|confirm|because|therefore|next|deadline|evidence)\b',
      caseSensitive: false,
    ).hasMatch(text)) {
      score += 20;
    }
    if (RegExp(
      r'\b(I think|maybe|thing|stuff)\b',
      caseSensitive: false,
    ).hasMatch(text)) {
      score -= 5;
    }
    return score.clamp(0, 100);
  }

  String _feedback(String text) {
    final parts = <String>[];
    if (!text.contains('?')) {
      parts.add('Add a focused clarification question.');
    }
    if (text.split(RegExp(r'\s+')).length < 12) {
      parts.add('Develop the answer with context and a clear action.');
    }
    if (!RegExp(
      r'\b(confirm|agree|send|review|deadline|next)\b',
      caseSensitive: false,
    ).hasMatch(text)) {
      parts.add('Close with an owner, action, or deadline.');
    }
    if (parts.isEmpty) {
      return 'Clear, purposeful language. Now improve precision by paraphrasing the partner’s key point.';
    }
    return parts.join(' ');
  }

  String _reply() {
    const replies = <String>[
      'I understand your point, but what specific evidence or commitment do you need from us?',
      'That may be possible. We need to consider cost, timing, and operational risk. Which point should we resolve first?',
      'I can offer a partial solution, although the deadline remains difficult. How would you like to document the next step?',
      'Before we close, please summarize the agreement, owner, and due date.',
    ];
    final index = turn < replies.length ? turn : replies.length - 1;
    return replies[index];
  }

  String _promptFor(int currentTurn) {
    const prompts = <String>[
      'Please clarify your priority and the evidence you need.',
      'A new constraint has appeared. The original deadline may not be possible.',
      'One participant disagrees and asks for a lower-cost alternative.',
      'Close the interaction with a precise summary and next actions.',
    ];
    final index = currentTurn < prompts.length
        ? currentTurn
        : prompts.length - 1;
    return prompts[index];
  }

  void _hint() {
    setState(() {
      feedback =
          'Hint: ${scenario.keyPhrases[turn % scenario.keyPhrases.length]}';
    });
  }
}

final class _Info extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Info(this.title, this.text);

  final String title;
  final String text;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 14),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: <Widget>[
          Text(title, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.titleSmall),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(text),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

final class _Bubble extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Bubble(this.label, this.text, this.mine);

  final String label;
  final String text;
  final bool mine;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Align(
      alignment: mine ? Alignment.centerRight : Alignment.centerLeft,
      child: Container(
        width: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width * 0.32,
        margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: mine
              ? Theme.of(context).colorScheme.primaryContainer
              : Theme.of(context).colorScheme.surface,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: <Widget>[
            Text(label, style: Theme.of(context).textTheme.labelSmall),
            const SizedBox(height: 5),
            Text(text),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

# 0.6.0+30 Two-Profile PIN Security

Visible product improvement:
- profile selector for exactly USER_1 and USER_2
- first sign-in creates a separate six-digit PIN per profile
- iterated HMAC-SHA256 PIN hashing with individual random salts
- credential records stored through Windows secure storage
- five wrong attempts lock only the selected profile for five minutes
- attempts reset after a correct PIN
- password text and the shared `change-me-now` credential removed

Evidence additions:
- separate-credential test
- non-plaintext hash test
- invalid-format test
- per-profile lockout and timed-unlock test

# 0.7.0+40 Startup Lock and Profile Switching

Visible updates aligned with the supplied application screenshots:
- PIN entry is mandatory after every application process start
- saved session ID is cleared during initialization
- prominent Switch profile action in the Today app bar
- Lock now action in a new active-profile security banner
- profile, goals, and learning-session providers are invalidated before switching
- current learner data remains learner-scoped and local
- existing adaptive plan, goals, profile, mastery, and progress screens are preserved

Evidence additions:
- saved-session startup-lock regression test
- sign-out/profile-switch regression test
- source-level verification for visible controls and provider invalidation

# 0.8.0+50 Learning Dashboard and Product Identity

Visible improvements based on the current application:
- Windows title changed from `english_acquisition_app` to `AI English Reflex Coach`
- new Learning insights page with learner-scoped mastery, completed sessions, curriculum size, today queue, priority review, and continue-learning action
- new Learning insights action in the Today app bar
- new Recommended setup panel for profile, goals, and learning evidence
- existing PIN-at-startup, profile switching, lock-now, local storage, adaptive plan, and learner isolation preserved

# 0.8.1+51 Learning Insights Data Contract Repair

- Fixes the Gate 4 analyzer failure from 0.8.0.
- Extends MasteryRecord with the real SQLite `attempts` value.
- Maps `activity_mastery.attempts` into the domain model instead of hiding the field.
- Adds an in-memory SQLite regression test proving two recorded attempts are exposed as `attempts == 2`.
- Preserves the Learning insights dashboard, Recommended setup, AI English Reflex Coach Windows title, startup PIN, profile switching, local data isolation, backup, and rollback.

$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
function Invoke-NativeCommand {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Executable,[Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string[]]$ArgumentList)
  $previous=$ErrorActionPreference
  try { $script:ErrorActionPreference='Continue'; & $Executable @ArgumentList; $code=$LASTEXITCODE }
  finally { $script:ErrorActionPreference=$previous }
  if($code -ne 0){ throw "Command failed with exit code ${code}: $Executable $($ArgumentList -join ' ')" }
}
function Build-WindowsWithRecovery {
  param([Parameter(Mandatory=$true)][string]$Mode)
  try { Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('build','windows',"--$Mode") }
  catch {
    Write-Warning "Windows $Mode build encountered stale MSBuild state. Cleaning generated build state and retrying once."
    Remove-Item '.\build\windows' -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Remove-Item '.\.dart_tool\flutter_build' -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('pub','get')
    Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('build','windows',"--$Mode")
  }
}
if(-not(Get-Command flutter -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)){throw 'Flutter is not available in PATH.'}
if(-not(Test-Path '.\pubspec.yaml')){throw "Run from the project root. Current folder: $(Get-Location)"}
Write-Host 'Checking Flutter and Windows toolchain...' -ForegroundColor Cyan
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('doctor','-v')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('config','--enable-windows-desktop')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('create','--platforms=windows','.')
Remove-Item '.\test\widget_test.dart' -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('pub','get')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'dart' -ArgumentList @('format','lib','test','tool')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'dart' -ArgumentList @('run','tool/validate_phase1.dart')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('analyze')
Invoke-NativeCommand -Executable 'flutter' -ArgumentList @('test')
Build-WindowsWithRecovery -Mode 'debug'
Build-WindowsWithRecovery -Mode 'release'
Write-Host 'Verification completed successfully.' -ForegroundColor Green
& '.\scripts\install_desktop_shortcut.ps1'
$exe='.\build\windows\x64\runner\Release\english_acquisition_app.exe'
if(-not(Test-Path $exe)){throw "Release executable not found: $exe"}
Start-Process -FilePath $exe

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"

$source = "C:\Users\forma\Desktop\Projects\english_acquisition_app_phase2_profile_goals"
$staging = "C:\Users\forma\Desktop\Projects\english_acquisition_app_v0.2.1_phase2_verified_source"
$zip = "C:\Users\forma\Desktop\Projects\english_acquisition_app_v0.2.1_phase2_verified_source.zip"

if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $source "pubspec.yaml"))) {
    throw "Kaynak proje bulunamadi: $source"
}

Remove-Item $staging -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
Remove-Item $zip -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $staging -Force | Out-Null

$sourceDirectories = @("lib", "test", "tool", "scripts", "assets", "docs")

foreach ($directory in $sourceDirectories) {
    $directorySource = Join-Path $source $directory
    $directoryTarget = Join-Path $staging $directory

    if (Test-Path $directorySource) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $directorySource -Destination $directoryTarget -Recurse -Force
    }
}

$windowsSource = Join-Path $source "windows"
$windowsTarget = Join-Path $staging "windows"

if (Test-Path $windowsSource) {
    Copy-Item -LiteralPath $windowsSource -Destination $windowsTarget -Recurse -Force
}

$rootFiles = @(
    ".gitignore",
    "analysis_options.yaml",
    "pubspec.yaml",
    "pubspec.lock",
    "README.md",
    "DEPENDENCY_RULES.md",
    "PHASE1_STATUS.md",
    "PHASE2_PLAN.md",
    "KNOWN_ISSUES.md",
    "WINDOWS_SETUP.md",
    "CHANGELOG.md"
)

foreach ($fileName in $rootFiles) {
    $fileSource = Join-Path $source $fileName
    if (Test-Path $fileSource) {
        Copy-Item -LiteralPath $fileSource -Destination (Join-Path $staging $fileName) -Force
    }
}

$unwantedDirectoryNames = @(
    ".dart_tool",
    "build",
    ".idea",
    ".git",
    "coverage",
    "ephemeral",
    "Debug",
    "Release",
    "RelWithDebInfo"
)

Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $staging -Recurse -Directory -Force |
    Where-Object { $_.Name -in $unwantedDirectoryNames } |
    Sort-Object { $_.FullName.Length } -Descending |
    ForEach-Object {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }

$unwantedExtensions = @(".exe", ".dll", ".pdb", ".ilk", ".obj", ".lib", ".exp")

Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $staging -Recurse -File -Force |
    Where-Object {
        $_.Name -like "verification_*.txt" -or
        $_.Name -eq "flutter_project_dump.txt" -or
        $_.Extension -in $unwantedExtensions
    } |
    ForEach-Object {
        Remove-Item -LiteralPath $_.FullName -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    }

if (-not (Test-Path (Join-Path $staging "pubspec.yaml"))) {
    throw "Temiz kaynak klasorunde pubspec.yaml bulunamadi."
}

Compress-Archive -Path (Join-Path $staging "*") -DestinationPath $zip -CompressionLevel Optimal -Force

if (-not (Test-Path $zip)) {
    throw "ZIP olusturulamadi: $zip"
}

$zipInfo = Get-Item $zip
$sizeMB = [Math]::Round($zipInfo.Length / 1MB, 2)

Write-Host ""
Write-Host "Temiz kaynak ZIP basariyla olusturuldu." -ForegroundColor Green
Write-Host "Dosya: $($zipInfo.FullName)" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Boyut: $sizeMB MB" -ForegroundColor Cyan
Write-Host "Bayt: $($zipInfo.Length)" -ForegroundColor Cyan

if ($zipInfo.Length -ge 25MB) {
    Write-Warning "ZIP hala 25 MB veya daha buyuk. En buyuk dosyalar listeleniyor:"
    Get-ChildItem -LiteralPath $staging -Recurse -File |
        Sort-Object Length -Descending |
        Select-Object -First 20 FullName, Length |
        Format-Table -AutoSize
    exit 2
}

Write-Host "ZIP GitHub web yukleme sinirinin altinda." -ForegroundColor Green

$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'
$projectRoot=(Resolve-Path (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot '..')).Path
$exe=Join-Path $projectRoot 'build\windows\x64\runner\Release\english_acquisition_app.exe'
if(-not(Test-Path $exe)){throw "Release application not found: $exe"}
$shortcutPath=Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('Desktop')) 'English Acquisition App.lnk'
$shell=New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell
$shortcut=$shell.CreateShortcut($shortcutPath)
$shortcut.TargetPath=$exe
$shortcut.WorkingDirectory=(Split-Path $exe)

param([string]$UpdateZip)
$ErrorActionPreference = 'Stop'
Add-Type -AssemblyName System.Windows.Forms
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($UpdateZip)) {
  $dialog = New-Object System.Windows.Forms.OpenFileDialog
  $dialog.Filter = 'Complete update ZIP (*.zip)|*.zip'
  $dialog.Title = 'Select English Acquisition App complete update'
  if ($dialog.ShowDialog() -ne [System.Windows.Forms.DialogResult]::OK) { return }
  $UpdateZip = $dialog.FileName
}
if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($UpdateZip)) {
  throw 'No update ZIP was selected.'
}
if (-not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $UpdateZip -PathType Leaf)) {
  throw "Update ZIP was not found: $UpdateZip"
}
$UpdateZip = (Resolve-Path -LiteralPath $UpdateZip).Path

$projects = Join-Path ([Environment]::GetFolderPath('Desktop')) 'Projects'
$current = Join-Path $projects 'english_acquisition_app_current'
$stage = Join-Path $projects '_english_acquisition_app_stage'
$rollback = Join-Path $projects '_english_acquisition_app_rollback'
$runtime = Join-Path $env:APPDATA 'EnglishAcquisitionApp\data'
$dataBackupRoot = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'EnglishAcquisitionAppBackups\data'
$logRoot = Join-Path $env:LOCALAPPDATA 'EnglishAcquisitionAppLogs'
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $logRoot,$dataBackupRoot -Force | Out-Null
Remove-Item $stage,$rollback -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Path $stage -Force | Out-Null
$stamp = Get-Date -Format 'yyyyMMdd_HHmmss'
$transcriptLog = Join-Path $logRoot ("update_$stamp.log")
$nativeLog = Join-Path $logRoot ("update_${stamp}_native.log")
$dataBackup = Join-Path $dataBackupRoot ("data_$stamp")
Start-Transcript -Path $transcriptLog -Force | Out-Null

function Invoke-NativeChecked {
  param([string]$Executable,[string[]]$ArgumentList,[string]$WorkingDirectory)
  Push-Location $WorkingDirectory
  $oldPreference = $ErrorActionPreference
  try {
    $script:ErrorActionPreference = 'Continue'
    & $Executable @ArgumentList 2>&1 | Tee-Object -FilePath $nativeLog -Append | Out-Host
    $exitCode = $LASTEXITCODE
  } finally {
    $script:ErrorActionPreference = $oldPreference
    Pop-Location
  }
  if ($exitCode -ne 0) {
    throw "$Executable $($ArgumentList -join ' ') failed with exit code $exitCode"
  }
}

try {
  Expand-Archive -LiteralPath $UpdateZip -DestinationPath $stage -Force
  $pubspec = Get-ChildItem $stage -Recurse -Filter pubspec.yaml -File |
    Where-Object { $_.FullName -notmatch '[\\/](build|\.dart_tool)[\\/]' } |
    Select-Object -First 1
  if ($null -eq $pubspec) { throw 'Selected ZIP does not contain a Flutter project.' }
  $source = Split-Path $pubspec.FullName

  Remove-Item (Join-Path $source 'pubspec.lock') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item (Join-Path $source '.dart_tool') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item (Join-Path $source 'build') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

  Write-Host 'Gate 1/7: dependency resolution'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'flutter' @('pub','get') $source
  Write-Host 'Gate 2/7: deterministic formatting and verification'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'dart' @('format','lib','test','tool') $source
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'dart' @('format','--output=none','--set-exit-if-changed','lib','test','tool') $source
  Write-Host 'Gate 3/7: V7 static audit'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'dart' @('run','tool/v7_release_audit.dart') $source
  Write-Host 'Gate 4/7: flutter analyze'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'flutter' @('analyze') $source
  Write-Host 'Gate 5/7: full automated tests'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'flutter' @('test') $source

  if (Test-Path $current) {
    robocopy $current $rollback /MIR /R:1 /W:1 /XD .dart_tool /XF *.log *.tmp | Out-Host
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ge 8) { throw "Source rollback snapshot failed: $LASTEXITCODE" }
  }
  if (Test-Path $runtime) {
    robocopy $runtime $dataBackup /MIR /R:1 /W:1 | Out-Host
    if ($LASTEXITCODE -ge 8) { throw "User data backup failed: $LASTEXITCODE" }
  }
  Get-Process english_acquisition_app -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue |
    Stop-Process -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

  robocopy $source $current /MIR /R:1 /W:1 /XD .git .idea coverage .dart_tool build /XF *.log *.tmp | Out-Host
  if ($LASTEXITCODE -ge 8) { throw "Source deployment failed: $LASTEXITCODE" }

  Remove-Item (Join-Path $current 'pubspec.lock') -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item (Join-Path $current '.dart_tool') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item (Join-Path $current 'build') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Remove-Item (Join-Path $current 'windows\flutter\ephemeral') -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue

  Invoke-NativeChecked 'flutter' @('pub','get') $current
  Write-Host 'Gate 6/7: flutter build windows --release'
  Invoke-NativeChecked 'flutter' @('build','windows','--release') $current
  $exe = Join-Path $current 'build\windows\x64\runner\Release\english_acquisition_app.exe'
  if (-not (Test-Path $exe)) { throw 'Release executable was not created.' }

  Write-Host 'Gate 7/7: application smoke launch'
  $smoke = Start-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory (Split-Path $exe) -PassThru
  Start-Sleep -Seconds 8
  if ($smoke.HasExited -and $smoke.ExitCode -ne 0) {
    throw "Application smoke test failed with exit code $($smoke.ExitCode)"
  }
  if (-not $smoke.HasExited) { Stop-Process -Id $smoke.Id -Force }

  & (Join-Path $current 'scripts\install_desktop_shortcuts.ps1')
  Remove-Item $rollback -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
  Start-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory (Split-Path $exe)
  Write-Host 'UPDATE SUCCESS - ALL V7 RELEASE GATES PASSED' -ForegroundColor Green
} catch {
  Write-Host 'UPDATE FAILED. Restoring source, build, and user data.' -ForegroundColor Red
  Write-Host $_.Exception.Message -ForegroundColor Red
  if (Test-Path $rollback) {
    Remove-Item $current -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    robocopy $rollback $current /MIR /R:1 /W:1 | Out-Host
  }
  if (Test-Path $dataBackup) {
    Remove-Item $runtime -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    robocopy $dataBackup $runtime /MIR /R:1 /W:1 | Out-Host
  }
  $oldExe = Join-Path $current 'build\windows\x64\runner\Release\english_acquisition_app.exe'
  if (Test-Path $oldExe) {
    Start-Process -FilePath $oldExe -WorkingDirectory (Split-Path $oldExe)
  }
  Write-Host 'ROLLBACK SUCCESS' -ForegroundColor Yellow
  Write-Host "Transcript log: $transcriptLog" -ForegroundColor Yellow
  Write-Host "Native tools log: $nativeLog" -ForegroundColor Yellow
  Read-Host 'Press Enter to close'
} finally {
  try { Stop-Transcript | Out-Null } catch {}
  Remove-Item $stage,$rollback -Recurse -Force -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learning_session/application/learning_session_controller.dart';

        c = LearningSessionController(LearningSessionRepository(db), 'one');
    c.load();
    c.start();
    expect(c.queue, hasLength(8));
    expect(c.check('RFQ'), isTrue);
    c.next();
    expect(c.mastery, isNotEmpty);
    final o = LearningSessionController(LearningSessionRepository(db), 'two');
    o.load();
    expect(o.mastery, isEmpty);
    db.close();
  });

BOYUT: 3375 bayt

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/pin_hasher.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_credential_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_session_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/data/repositories/local_two_user_authentication_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/domain/failures/authentication_failure.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

final class MemorySessionStore implements SecureSessionStore {
  String? value;
  @override
  Future<void> clear() async => value = null;
  @override
  Future<String?> readUserId() async => value;
  @override
  Future<void> writeUserId(String userId) async => value = userId;
}

final class MemoryCredentialStore implements SecureCredentialStore {
  final values = <String, StoredCredential>{};
  @override
  Future<StoredCredential?> read(String userId) async => values[userId];
  @override
  Future<void> write(String userId, StoredCredential credential) async =>
      values[userId] = credential;
}

void main() {
  test('first sign-in creates separate hashed credentials', () async {
    final credentials = MemoryCredentialStore();
    final repository = LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
      MemorySessionStore(),
      credentials,
      pinHasher: const PinHasher(iterations: 10),
    );
    expect(
      (await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123456')).id,
      'learner-1',
    );
    expect(
      (await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_2', password: '654321')).id,
      'learner-2',
    );
    expect(credentials.values.length, 2);
    expect(credentials.values['learner-1']!.hash, isNot('123456'));
    expect(
      credentials.values['learner-1']!.hash,
      isNot(credentials.values['learner-2']!.hash),
    );
  });

  test('invalid PIN format is rejected', () async {
    final repository = LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
      MemorySessionStore(),
      MemoryCredentialStore(),
      pinHasher: const PinHasher(iterations: 10),
    );
    expect(
      () => repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123'),
      throwsA(isA<AuthenticationFailure>()),
    );
  });

  test('five incorrect PINs lock only selected profile', () async {
    var now = DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 4, 12);
    final credentials = MemoryCredentialStore();
    final repository = LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
      MemorySessionStore(),
      credentials,
      pinHasher: const PinHasher(iterations: 10),
      clock: () => now,
    );
    await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123456');
    await repository.signOut();
    for (var attempt = 0; attempt < 5; attempt++) {
      try {
        await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '000000');
      } on AuthenticationFailure {
        // Expected while exercising the lockout counter.
      }
    }
    expect(
      () => repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123456'),
      throwsA(isA<AuthenticationFailure>()),
    );
    expect(
      (await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_2', password: '654321')).id,
      'learner-2',
    );
    now = now.add(const Duration(minutes: 6));
    expect(
      (await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123456')).id,
      'learner-1',
    );
  });
}

import 'dart:io';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/backup/local_backup_service.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('database backup and restore preserve bytes', () async {
    final temp = await Directory.systemTemp.createTemp('backup_restore_');
    addTearDown(() => temp.delete(recursive: true));
    final database = File('${temp.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}app.sqlite3');
    await database.writeAsBytes([1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
    final service = LocalBackupService();
    final backup = await service.createDatabaseBackup(
      databaseFile: database,
      backupDirectory: Directory('${temp.path}${Platform.pathSeparator}backup'),
      now: DateTime.utc(2026, 11, 3, 19, 0),
    );
    expect(backup, isNotNull);
    await database.writeAsBytes([9]);
    await service.restoreDatabaseBackup(
      backupFile: backup!,
      databaseFile: database,
    );
    expect(await database.readAsBytes(), [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
  });
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learner_profile/application/controllers/learner_profile_controller.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learner_profile/data/repositories/sqlite_learner_profile_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learner_profile/domain/entities/learner_profile.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('incomplete profile is rejected', () async {
    final db = LocalDatabase.inMemory();
    final c = LearnerProfileController(
      SqliteLearnerProfileRepository(db),
      'learner-1',
    );
    final result = await c.save(
      LearnerProfile(
        accessibilityNotes: '',
        communicationTasks: '',
        displayName: '',
        englishLevel: 'B1',
        industry: '',
        learnerId: 'learner-1',
        nativeLanguage: '',
        occupationalRole: '',
        preferredSessionMinutes: 25,
        procurementExperienceYears: 0,
        updatedAt: DateTime.now(),
      ),
    );
    expect(result, isFalse);
    expect(c.errorMessage, isNotNull);
    db.close();
  });

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learning_session/curriculum.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learning_session/learning_session.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('mastery records expose persisted attempt counts', () {
    final database = LocalDatabase.inMemory();
    addTearDown(database.close);
    const learnerId = 'learner-1';
    const repositoryActivity = ProcurementCurriculum.activities;
    final repository = LearningSessionRepository(database);
    final activity = repositoryActivity.first;
    repository.record(learnerId, activity, 70);
    repository.record(learnerId, activity, 90);
    final mastery = repository.mastery(learnerId).single;
    expect(mastery.activityId, activity.id);
    expect(mastery.attempts, 2);
    expect(mastery.mastery, greaterThan(0));
  });
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/goals/data/repositories/sqlite_goals_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/goals/domain/entities/learner_goal.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learner_profile/data/repositories/sqlite_learner_profile_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/learner_profile/domain/entities/learner_profile.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('profiles and goals are isolated by learner id', () async {
    final db = LocalDatabase.inMemory();
    final profiles = SqliteLearnerProfileRepository(db);
    final goals = SqliteGoalsRepository(db);
    final now = DateTime.utc(2026, 1, 1);
    await profiles.save(
      LearnerProfile(
        accessibilityNotes: '',
        communicationTasks: 'Supplier meetings',
        displayName: 'Furkan',
        englishLevel: 'B1',
        industry: 'Automotive',
        learnerId: 'learner-1',
        nativeLanguage: 'Turkish',
        occupationalRole: 'Procurement',
        preferredSessionMinutes: 25,
        procurementExperienceYears: 1,
        updatedAt: now,
      ),
    );
    await goals.save(
      LearnerGoal(
        createdAt: now,
        description: '',
        id: 'g1',
        learnerId: 'learner-1',
        occupationalContext: 'RFQ',
        priority: 1,
        status: GoalStatus.active,
        title: 'Clarify RFQs',
        updatedAt: now,
      ),
    );
    expect((await profiles.load('learner-1'))?.displayName, 'Furkan');
    expect(await profiles.load('learner-2'), isNull);
    expect((await goals.load('learner-1')).length, 1);
    expect(await goals.load('learner-2'), isEmpty);
    db.close();
  });
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/pin_hasher.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_credential_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_session_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/data/repositories/local_two_user_authentication_repository.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test(
    'startup session clear cannot invalidate a correct existing PIN',
    () async {
      final sessions = VolatileSecureSessionStore();
      final credentials = _MemoryCredentialStore();
      final repository = LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
        sessions,
        credentials,
        pinHasher: const PinHasher(),
      );

      await repository.signIn(userName: 'USER_2', password: '246810');
      await sessions.clear();

      final user = await repository.signIn(
        userName: 'USER_2',
        password: '246810',
      );

      expect(user.id, 'learner-2');
      expect(await sessions.readUserId(), 'learner-2');
    },
  );
}

final class _MemoryCredentialStore implements SecureCredentialStore {
  final Map<String, StoredCredential> _values = <String, StoredCredential>{};

  @override
  Future<StoredCredential?> read(String userId) async => _values[userId];

  @override
  Future<void> write(String userId, StoredCredential credential) async {
    _values[userId] = credential;
  }
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/pin_hasher.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_credential_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/security/secure_session_store.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/application/controllers/authentication_controller.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/data/repositories/local_two_user_authentication_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/domain/value_objects/authentication_status.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

final class MemorySessionStore implements SecureSessionStore {
  String? value = 'learner-1';
  @override
  Future<void> clear() async => value = null;
  @override
  Future<String?> readUserId() async => value;
  @override
  Future<void> writeUserId(String userId) async => value = userId;
}

final class MemoryCredentialStore implements SecureCredentialStore {
  final values = <String, StoredCredential>{};
  @override
  Future<StoredCredential?> read(String userId) async => values[userId];
  @override
  Future<void> write(String userId, StoredCredential value) async =>
      values[userId] = value;
}

void main() {
  test('startup clears a saved session and requires PIN', () async {
    final session = MemorySessionStore();
    final controller = AuthenticationController(
      LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
        session,
        MemoryCredentialStore(),
        pinHasher: const PinHasher(iterations: 10),
      ),
    );
    await controller.initialize();
    expect(session.value, isNull);
    expect(controller.user, isNull);
    expect(controller.status, AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated);
  });

  test('switching profile clears current session', () async {
    final session = MemorySessionStore();
    final controller = AuthenticationController(
      LocalTwoUserAuthenticationRepository(
        session,
        MemoryCredentialStore(),
        pinHasher: const PinHasher(iterations: 10),
      ),
    );
    await controller.initialize();
    await controller.signIn(userName: 'USER_1', password: '123456');
    expect(controller.user?.id, 'learner-1');
    await controller.signOut();
    expect(session.value, isNull);
    expect(controller.status, AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated);
  });
}

import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:io';

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/application/controllers/authentication_controller.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/domain/entities/authenticated_user.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/domain/repositories/authentication_repository.dart';
import 'package:english_acquisition_app/features/authentication/domain/value_objects/authentication_status.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

final class NeverCompletingSignOutRepository
    implements AuthenticationRepository {
  final Completer<void> signOutCompleter = Completer<void>();

  @override
  Future<AuthenticatedUser?> restore() async => null;

  @override
  Future<AuthenticatedUser> signIn({
    required String password,
    required String userName,
  }) async => const AuthenticatedUser(displayName: 'Furkan', id: 'learner-1');

  @override
  Future<void> signOut() => signOutCompleter.future;
}

void main() {
  test(
    'startup unlocks UI without waiting for secure storage cleanup',
    () async {
      final controller = AuthenticationController(
        NeverCompletingSignOutRepository(),
      );
      addTearDown(controller.dispose);

      await controller.initialize();

      expect(controller.status, AuthenticationStatus.unauthenticated);
      expect(controller.user, isNull);
      expect(controller.isBusy, isFalse);
    },
  );

  test('router has no startup spinner route', () {
    final source = File('lib/app/routing/app_router.dart').readAsStringSync();
    expect(source, contains("initialLocation: '/sign-in'"));
    expect(source, isNot(contains("path: '/starting'")));
    expect(source, isNot(contains('CircularProgressIndicator')));
  });
}

import 'package:english_acquisition_app/core/persistence/local_database.dart';
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';

void main() {
  test('current schema keeps every learner-owned table scoped', () {
    final database = LocalDatabase.inMemory();
    expect(database.raw.userVersion, LocalDatabase.currentSchemaVersion);
    for (final table in <String>[
      'learner_profiles',
      'learner_goals',
      'learning_sessions',
      'practice_responses',
      'activity_mastery',
      'session_activities',
    ]) {
      final columns = database.raw
          .select('PRAGMA table_info($table)')
          .map((row) => row['name'] as String)
          .toSet();
      expect(
        columns,
        contains('learner_id'),
        reason: '$table must be learner-scoped',
      );
    }
    database.close();
  });
}

import 'dart:io';

void main() {
  final failures = <String>[];
  for (final path in <String>[
    'lib/core/backup/local_backup_service.dart',
    'lib/core/security/pin_hasher.dart',
    'lib/core/security/secure_credential_store.dart',
    'test/authentication_security_test.dart',
    'test/startup_lock_and_switch_test.dart',
    'test/home_security_controls_test.dart',
    'test/learning_insights_ui_test.dart',
    'test/mastery_attempts_contract_test.dart',
    'lib/features/learning_session/presentation/learning_insights_page.dart',
    'test/backup_restore_test.dart',
    'test/v7_schema_isolation_test.dart',
    'scripts/update_from_zip.ps1',
    'scripts/install_desktop_shortcuts.ps1',
    'windows/runner/resources/app_icon.ico',
  ]) {
    if (!File(path).existsSync()) failures.add('Missing $path');
  }
  final pubspec = File('pubspec.yaml').readAsStringSync();
  for (final token in <String>['http:', 'dio:', 'firebase_', 'supabase']) {
    if (pubspec.contains(token)) failures.add('Online dependency $token');
  }
  final updater = File('scripts/update_from_zip.ps1').readAsStringSync();
  for (final token in <String>[
    "@('analyze')",
    "@('test')",
    "@('build','windows','--release')",
    'ROLLBACK SUCCESS',
    'ALL V7 RELEASE GATES PASSED',
  ]) {
    if (!updater.contains(token)) failures.add('Updater gate $token');
  }
  if (failures.isNotEmpty) {
    stderr.writeln(failures.join('\n'));
    exitCode = 1;
  } else {
    stdout.writeln('V7 static audit passed.');
  }
}

# Windows Setup and Acceptance

## Required tools

- Flutter stable
- Git for Windows
- Visual Studio with Desktop development with C++

## Commands

Full setup and verification:

```powershell
Set-ExecutionPolicy -Scope Process Bypass
.\scripts\bootstrap_verify_and_run_windows.ps1
```

Development run with the Flutter 3.47 Windows Impeller workaround:

```powershell
.\scripts\run_windows.ps1
```

Stable release run:

```powershell
.\scripts\run_windows_release.ps1
```

## Acceptance checklist

- invalid credentials are rejected
- `furkan` signs in
- `learner2` signs in
- the currently authenticated user is restored after restart
- sign-out clears the session
- release build remains open

The Android SDK and Chrome are not required for Windows-only Phase 1 verification.

# Project-level configuration.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.14)
project(english_acquisition_app LANGUAGES CXX)

# The name of the executable created for the application. Change this to change
# the on-disk name of your application.
set(BINARY_NAME "english_acquisition_app")

# Explicitly opt in to modern CMake behaviors to avoid warnings with recent
# versions of CMake.
cmake_policy(VERSION 3.14...3.25)

# Define build configuration option.
get_property(IS_MULTICONFIG GLOBAL PROPERTY GENERATOR_IS_MULTI_CONFIG)
if(IS_MULTICONFIG)
  set(CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES "Debug;Profile;Release"
    CACHE STRING "" FORCE)
else()
  if(NOT CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE AND NOT CMAKE_CONFIGURATION_TYPES)
    set(CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE "Debug" CACHE
      STRING "Flutter build mode" FORCE)
    set_property(CACHE CMAKE_BUILD_TYPE PROPERTY STRINGS
      "Debug" "Profile" "Release")
  endif()
endif()
# Define settings for the Profile build mode.
set(CMAKE_EXE_LINKER_FLAGS_PROFILE "${CMAKE_EXE_LINKER_FLAGS_RELEASE}")
set(CMAKE_SHARED_LINKER_FLAGS_PROFILE "${CMAKE_SHARED_LINKER_FLAGS_RELEASE}")
set(CMAKE_C_FLAGS_PROFILE "${CMAKE_C_FLAGS_RELEASE}")
set(CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS_PROFILE "${CMAKE_CXX_FLAGS_RELEASE}")

# Use Unicode for all projects.
add_definitions(-DUNICODE -D_UNICODE)

# Compilation settings that should be applied to most targets.
#
# Be cautious about adding new options here, as plugins use this function by
# default. In most cases, you should add new options to specific targets instead
# of modifying this function.
function(APPLY_STANDARD_SETTINGS TARGET)
  target_compile_features(${TARGET} PUBLIC cxx_std_17)
  target_compile_options(${TARGET} PRIVATE /W4 /WX /wd"4100")
  target_compile_options(${TARGET} PRIVATE /EHsc)
  target_compile_definitions(${TARGET} PRIVATE "_HAS_EXCEPTIONS=0")
  target_compile_definitions(${TARGET} PRIVATE "$<$<CONFIG:Debug>:_DEBUG>")
endfunction()

# Flutter library and tool build rules.
set(FLUTTER_MANAGED_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/flutter")
add_subdirectory(${FLUTTER_MANAGED_DIR})

# Application build; see runner/CMakeLists.txt.
add_subdirectory("runner")


# Generated plugin build rules, which manage building the plugins and adding
# them to the application.
include(flutter/generated_plugins.cmake)


# === Installation ===
# Support files are copied into place next to the executable, so that it can
# run in place. This is done instead of making a separate bundle (as on Linux)
# so that building and running from within Visual Studio will work.
set(BUILD_BUNDLE_DIR "$<TARGET_FILE_DIR:${BINARY_NAME}>")
# Make the "install" step default, as it's required to run.
set(CMAKE_VS_INCLUDE_INSTALL_TO_DEFAULT_BUILD 1)
if(CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX_INITIALIZED_TO_DEFAULT)
  set(CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX "${BUILD_BUNDLE_DIR}" CACHE PATH "..." FORCE)
endif()

set(INSTALL_BUNDLE_DATA_DIR "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}/data")
set(INSTALL_BUNDLE_LIB_DIR "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}")

install(TARGETS ${BINARY_NAME} RUNTIME DESTINATION "${CMAKE_INSTALL_PREFIX}"
  COMPONENT Runtime)

install(FILES "${FLUTTER_ICU_DATA_FILE}" DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_DATA_DIR}"
  COMPONENT Runtime)

install(FILES "${FLUTTER_LIBRARY}" DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_LIB_DIR}"
  COMPONENT Runtime)

if(PLUGIN_BUNDLED_LIBRARIES)
  install(FILES "${PLUGIN_BUNDLED_LIBRARIES}"
    DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_LIB_DIR}"
    COMPONENT Runtime)
endif()

# Copy the native assets provided by the build.dart from all packages.
set(NATIVE_ASSETS_DIR "${PROJECT_BUILD_DIR}native_assets/windows/")
install(DIRECTORY "${NATIVE_ASSETS_DIR}"
   DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_LIB_DIR}"
   COMPONENT Runtime)

# Fully re-copy the assets directory on each build to avoid having stale files
# from a previous install.
set(FLUTTER_ASSET_DIR_NAME "flutter_assets")
install(CODE "
  file(REMOVE_RECURSE \"${INSTALL_BUNDLE_DATA_DIR}/${FLUTTER_ASSET_DIR_NAME}\")
  " COMPONENT Runtime)
install(DIRECTORY "${PROJECT_BUILD_DIR}/${FLUTTER_ASSET_DIR_NAME}"
  DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_DATA_DIR}" COMPONENT Runtime)

# Install the AOT library on non-Debug builds only.
install(FILES "${AOT_LIBRARY}" DESTINATION "${INSTALL_BUNDLE_DATA_DIR}"
  CONFIGURATIONS Profile;Release
  COMPONENT Runtime)

# This file controls Flutter-level build steps. It should not be edited.
cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.14)

set(EPHEMERAL_DIR "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/ephemeral")

# Configuration provided via flutter tool.
include(${EPHEMERAL_DIR}/generated_config.cmake)

# TODO: Move the rest of this into files in ephemeral. See
# https://github.com/flutter/flutter/issues/57146.
set(WRAPPER_ROOT "${EPHEMERAL_DIR}/cpp_client_wrapper")

# Set fallback configurations for older versions of the flutter tool.
if (NOT DEFINED FLUTTER_TARGET_PLATFORM)
  set(FLUTTER_TARGET_PLATFORM "windows-x64")
endif()

# === Flutter Library ===
set(FLUTTER_LIBRARY "${EPHEMERAL_DIR}/flutter_windows.dll")

# Published to parent scope for install step.
set(FLUTTER_LIBRARY ${FLUTTER_LIBRARY} PARENT_SCOPE)
set(FLUTTER_ICU_DATA_FILE "${EPHEMERAL_DIR}/icudtl.dat" PARENT_SCOPE)
set(PROJECT_BUILD_DIR "${PROJECT_DIR}/build/" PARENT_SCOPE)
set(AOT_LIBRARY "${PROJECT_DIR}/build/windows/app.so" PARENT_SCOPE)

list(APPEND FLUTTER_LIBRARY_HEADERS
  "flutter_export.h"
  "flutter_windows.h"
  "flutter_messenger.h"
  "flutter_plugin_registrar.h"
  "flutter_texture_registrar.h"
)
list(TRANSFORM FLUTTER_LIBRARY_HEADERS PREPEND "${EPHEMERAL_DIR}/")
add_library(flutter INTERFACE)
target_include_directories(flutter INTERFACE
  "${EPHEMERAL_DIR}"
)
target_link_libraries(flutter INTERFACE "${FLUTTER_LIBRARY}.lib")
add_dependencies(flutter flutter_assemble)

# === Wrapper ===
list(APPEND CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE
  "core_implementations.cc"
  "standard_codec.cc"
)
list(TRANSFORM CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE PREPEND "${WRAPPER_ROOT}/")
list(APPEND CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_PLUGIN
  "plugin_registrar.cc"
)
list(TRANSFORM CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_PLUGIN PREPEND "${WRAPPER_ROOT}/")
list(APPEND CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_APP
  "flutter_engine.cc"
  "flutter_view_controller.cc"
)
list(TRANSFORM CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_APP PREPEND "${WRAPPER_ROOT}/")

# Wrapper sources needed for a plugin.
add_library(flutter_wrapper_plugin STATIC
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE}
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_PLUGIN}
)
apply_standard_settings(flutter_wrapper_plugin)
set_target_properties(flutter_wrapper_plugin PROPERTIES
  POSITION_INDEPENDENT_CODE ON)
set_target_properties(flutter_wrapper_plugin PROPERTIES
  CXX_VISIBILITY_PRESET hidden)
target_link_libraries(flutter_wrapper_plugin PUBLIC flutter)
target_include_directories(flutter_wrapper_plugin PUBLIC
  "${WRAPPER_ROOT}/include"
)
add_dependencies(flutter_wrapper_plugin flutter_assemble)

# Wrapper sources needed for the runner.
add_library(flutter_wrapper_app STATIC
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE}
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_APP}
)
apply_standard_settings(flutter_wrapper_app)
target_link_libraries(flutter_wrapper_app PUBLIC flutter)
target_include_directories(flutter_wrapper_app PUBLIC
  "${WRAPPER_ROOT}/include"
)
add_dependencies(flutter_wrapper_app flutter_assemble)

# === Flutter tool backend ===
# _phony_ is a non-existent file to force this command to run every time,
# since currently there's no way to get a full input/output list from the
# flutter tool.
set(PHONY_OUTPUT "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/_phony_")
set_source_files_properties("${PHONY_OUTPUT}" PROPERTIES SYMBOLIC TRUE)
add_custom_command(
  OUTPUT ${FLUTTER_LIBRARY} ${FLUTTER_LIBRARY_HEADERS}
    ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE} ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_PLUGIN}
    ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_APP}
    ${PHONY_OUTPUT}
  COMMAND ${CMAKE_COMMAND} -E env
    ${FLUTTER_TOOL_ENVIRONMENT}
    "${FLUTTER_ROOT}/packages/flutter_tools/bin/tool_backend.bat"
      ${FLUTTER_TARGET_PLATFORM} $<CONFIG>
  VERBATIM
)
add_custom_target(flutter_assemble DEPENDS
  "${FLUTTER_LIBRARY}"
  ${FLUTTER_LIBRARY_HEADERS}
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_CORE}
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_PLUGIN}
  ${CPP_WRAPPER_SOURCES_APP}
)

#ifndef FLUTTER_SHELL_PLATFORM_COMMON_CLIENT_WRAPPER_BINARY_MESSENGER_IMPL_H_
#define FLUTTER_SHELL_PLATFORM_COMMON_CLIENT_WRAPPER_BINARY_MESSENGER_IMPL_H_

#include <flutter_messenger.h>

#include <map>
#include <string>

#include "include/flutter/binary_messenger.h"

namespace flutter {

// Wrapper around a FlutterDesktopMessengerRef that implements the
// BinaryMessenger API.
class BinaryMessengerImpl : public BinaryMessenger {
 public:
  explicit BinaryMessengerImpl(FlutterDesktopMessengerRef core_messenger);

  virtual ~BinaryMessengerImpl();

  // Prevent copying.
  BinaryMessengerImpl(BinaryMessengerImpl const&) = delete;
  BinaryMessengerImpl& operator=(BinaryMessengerImpl const&) = delete;

  // |flutter::BinaryMessenger|
  void Send(const std::string& channel,
            const uint8_t* message,
            size_t message_size,
            BinaryReply reply) const override;

  // |flutter::BinaryMessenger|
  void SetMessageHandler(const std::string& channel,
                         BinaryMessageHandler handler) override;

 private:
  // Handle for interacting with the C API.
  FlutterDesktopMessengerRef messenger_;

  // A map from channel names to the BinaryMessageHandler that should be called
  // for incoming messages on that channel.
  std::map<std::string, BinaryMessageHandler> handlers_;
};

}  // namespace flutter

#endif  // FLUTTER_SHELL_PLATFORM_COMMON_CLIENT_WRAPPER_BINARY_MESSENGER_IMPL_H_
